(cek kuṭiyaracu)

					



iyal – 3

cemmoḻit tamiḻilakkiyac
(caṅka ilakkiyac) ciṟappu

tolkāppiyam

tolkāppiyam “māntaṉ aṟivāṟṟal evvaḷavu viyattaku ucca nili eytakkūṭum eṉpatik kāṭṭum ciṟanta cāṉṟukaḷuḷ oṉṟu” One of the finest monuments of human intelligence  . ilakkiyak koḷkiyil vēṟu entap paṇṭiya moḻikaḷilum illāta ciṟanta karuttukkaḷit tol poruḷ. ceyyuḷiyal kūṟukiṟatu.

kē.vi. cuvelepil

tolkāppiyac ceyyuḷiyal kōṭpāṭṭuc ciṟappu :

tolkāppiyam aṭippaṭi nūlāka iruppatu ilakkaṇa ilakkiya āyvukaḷukku maṭṭumaṉṟu; mānta iṉap paraval cārnta puviyiyal, kumukāya māntaviyal, paṇpāṭu, cūḻniliyiyal, uḷaviyal ākiya pulaṅkaḷ cārnta ariya ceytikaḷ annūlil uḷḷaṉa; eṉavē tamiḻar nākarikam- paṇpāṭu cārnta (ēṉ potuvāṉa mānta iṉa nākarikam- paṇpāṭu cārnta) āyvukaḷukkut tolkāppiyam mika iṉṟiyamiyātatu. eṉiṉum ilakkiyam kūṟuvatellām (akkālattil nilaviya) uṇmik kumukāya niliyi nēraṭiyākak kāṭṭukiṟatu eṉṟu eṇṇutal tavaṟu; uṇmi niliyi viruppuveṟuppiṉṟi aṟiviyal pūrvamākak kāṭṭukiṟatu eṉṟu eṇṇutal mēlum tavaṟākum. akkāla āṇ peṇmakkaḷiṉ akam, puṟa vāḻkkiyi uḷḷatu uḷḷapaṭi tatrupamākac caṅkap pulavarkaḷuṭiya pāṭalkaḷ kāṭṭuvatākak kumuka varalāṟṟāciriyar cilar muṉṉar koṇṭuḷḷaṉar. āṉāl uṇmi eṉṉa? ceyyuḷ ‘uṇmi vāḻkki” nikaḻccikaḷi antanta nikaḻcci eppaṭi irukka vēṇṭumeṉṟa ilakkiyamarapu uḷḷatō anta marapukkuk kaṭṭuppaṭṭē cittirikkiṟatu. atāvatu marapukkēṟṟa māṟṟaṅkaḷuṭaṉ tāṉ cittirikkiṟatu. “uṇmi vāḻkki” vēṟu; ati ilakkiyattil kāṭṭum marapu vēṟu; eṉpatip pulavarkaḷum poruḷilakkaṇavāciriyarum uṇarntiruntaṉar. uṇarntiruntataṉāltāṉ uṇmi vāḻkkiyiṉ kūṟṟukkaḷ ceykikaḷi ‘ulakiyal vaḻakku” eṉṟum ilakkiyattil (ceyyuḷil) vāḻkkiyic cittirikkum marapic ceyyuḷ vaḻakku “pulaṉeṟi vaḻakku” eṉṟum kuṟittaṉar. cila nikaḻccikaḷ, ceytikaḷi ippaṭi ippaṭittāṉ kāṭṭa vēṇṭum eṉṟu ilakkiyappaṭip pāḷarkaḷum vācakarkaḷum tamakkuḷ icintu uruvākkum mēlvaric caṭṭamākiya marapē ilakkiya marapākum. (e.kā. ovvoru tiṇikkum uriya mutal, karu, uripporuḷkaḷ). nikaḻcci/ ceyti vācakarukkut terintatu tāṉ; ataṉi oru putiya pārviyil, kōṇattil (marapi aṭippaṭiyil mīṟāmal) kāṭṭum poḻutu vācakar anta nayatti naṉku uṇarntu pārāṭṭa iyalkiṟatu. akappāṭal etuvum orutaṭavikūṭat talivaṉ, talivi peyaric cuṭṭu vatilli. talivaṉum taliviyum eppoḻutum iṉṉār eṉṟu kuṟippāka aṭiyāḷam kāṭṭa muṭiyātavarkaḷē. eṉavē akappāṭalkaḷ cittirikkum uṇarvukaḷ (ṭapiḷpu. ec. haṭcaṉ tamatu “ilakkiyap payiṟcikkāṉa muṉṉuri” 1946 nūlpakkam 97il colvatu pōla) “potuvāka enta āṭavaṉukkum peṇṇukkum poruntuvatāka irukkumēyaṉṟi ivaṉukkut tāṉ/ivaḷukkuttāṉ poruntum eṉṟu irukkātu. (uḷḷa uṇarvukaḷi viḷakkātu) veḷiyulaki viritturikkum puṟappāṭalkaḷil cila potuvāka aṉivarum nōkkum pārviyil irukkum; cila antantap pulavaruṭiya taṉi nōkkil amintatāka irukkum. piṉṉavaṟṟukku eṭuttuk kāṭṭu kiyaṟunilip pāṭalkaḷ. vaḷḷalkaḷ, naṇparkaḷ iṟantāl pulavarkaḷ tam neñciṉ āḻattiliruntu uṇarvukaḷik koṭṭip pāṭum tuyarap pāṭalkaḷ ovvoṉṟum taṉittaṉmi vāyntavi; potuvāṉa nāṭṭuppuṟa oppārikaḷiṉ vālāyap paṭimaṅkaḷik koṇṭaviyalla”.

kē.vi. cuvelepil

2. tirukkuṟaḷ

“intiya makkaḷiṭiyē paṇṭik kālattiliruntē piṟa uyirkaḷ aṉittukkum aṉpukāṭṭi utavum aṟak koḷkiṉa (the idea of active love) ” iruntu vantati, intiyap paṇṭiya ilakkiyaṅkaḷilum kāṇum katikaḷil iruntu aṟikiṟōm. attuṭaṉ kuṟippākat tirukkuṟaḷ eṉṉum nūlil kāṇum aṟavuric ceyyuḷkaḷiliruntum aṟikiṟōm.

“(ulakil piṟa uyirkaḷiṭattu aṉpu kāṭṭic ceyalpaṭuvati (life Affirmation) avvaḷavāka valiyuṟuttāta maṉutarma cāttiram pōṉṟavaṟṟiliruntu māṟupaṭum) kuṟaḷil ulakiyum vāḻkkiyiyum veṟuttu otukkum pōkku (World and life negation) paravalākak kāṇappaṭuvatu alla. eṅkō ōriru iṭattil vēṇṭumāṉāl irukkalām.

“pirāmaṇīyam, puttamatam pakavatkīti pōṉṟavaṟṟip pōl immi maṟumip payaṉ karutiyē aṟaneṟiyil niṟṟal vēṇṭum eṉṟa vāṇikak koḷkiyik kuṟaḷ valiyuṟuttuvatu illi. nallatu ceyvatē takkatu eṉṟa uṇarviṉālēyē nallatu (aṟam) ceyya vēṇṭum eṉṟu kuṟaḷ kūṟukiṟatu (kuṟaḷkaḷ 222,211 pakavatkītiyō karumam ceytu koṇṭiruppatu pirapañcaniyati eṉṟu vaṟaṭṭut taṉamāka valintu kūṟukiṟatu. āṉāl, eṉṉa viyappu, kuṟaḷō maṉitaṉ uḻippatum īṭṭuvatum piṟarukkum piṟa uyirkaḷukkum avaṉ naṉmi ceyvataṟkākattāṉ eṉṟu cāṟṟukiṟatu. (kuṟaḷkaḷ 81,212) pakavat kītippaṭi kaṭami jātikku takkapaṭi vēṟupaṭum. kuṟaḷō makkaḷ aṉivarum ‘nallavi ceytoḻukak kūṟukiṟatu’.

“ēṉi intiya tarmacāstirakkārar uḻippatil, muyaṟci ceyvatil makiḻvōṭu īṭupaṭu eṉṟu kūṟavilli. kuṟaḷkaḷ 619,630 pōṉṟaviyō ulakiyalilum, vāḻkkiyilum, ārvattuṭaṉ īṭupaṭum paṭiaṟivuṟuttukiṉṟaṉa.”

“(puttamum kītiyum pōlap paṟṟiṉmi, veṟuppiṉmi, kollāmi, akimci ākiyavaṟṟiyum kuṟaḷ valiyuṟuttiṉālum kūṭa atē camayattil) kuṟaḷ atikamāka valiyuṟuttuvatu aṉpum aruḷumē yām (the living ethic of love). kāṇka kuṟaḷkaḷ 72,78,79,103226,241.” “aṟaneṟicār ulaka vāḻkki ilaṭciyattit tiṟampaṭac cuṭṭukiṟatu kuṟaḷ. maṉitaṉiṉ taṉi vāḻkki. piṟa uyirkaḷōṭum ulakattōṭum uṟaḻum avaṉ vāḻkki ākiyavaṟṟil kikkoḷḷa vēṇṭiya neṟikaḷik kuṟaḷ viyattaku paṇpuṭaṉum cālpuṭaṉum naṭimuṟikku kanta vakiyil vakuttuḷḷatu ((characterised by nobility and good sense). ulaka ilakkiyattil vēṟu eṅkum ivvaḷavu ciṟanta oḷimayamāṉa aṟavuri vācakaṅkaḷi vaḻaṅkum nūlik kāṇa iyalātu; (kuṟaḷkaḷ 92, 105, 108, 121, 159, 162, 216, 298, 319, 578,594, 628, 757, 782, 874, 931, 973, 999, 1007, 1024, 1032”

“āka kiṟittuva ūḻit toṭakkak kālattilēyē intiya makkaḷiṭiyē ulakavāḻkkiyiṭamum uyirkaḷiṭamum paṇpum aṉpum kāṭṭuma oru taṉmi iyalpāka iruntu vantuḷḷatik kuṟaḷiliruntu aṟikiṟōm. pirāmaṇiyam, puttam, pakavatkīti cār intu matam ākiyavaṟṟiṉ kōṭpāṭukaḷil illātatu atu. āṉāl kīḻcātiyiṉariṭamum, cātāraṇa makkaḷiṭamum tōṉṟi makkaḷōṭu makkaḷāka vāḻnta ciṟanta camayap pōtakarkaḷ mūlamāka attaṉmi metumetuvāka nāḷaṭivil intu matattil iṭampeṟalāyiṟṟu.” 

- ālparṭ cuviṭcar: intiyac cintaṉiyum ataṉ vaḷarcciyum Indian Thought and its development; 1936

3. eṭṭuttoki, pattuppāṭṭiṉ ciṟappukaḷ (vēṟu entac cemmoḻi ilakkiyattukkum illātavi) “eṭṭuttoki, pattuppāṭṭu nūlkaḷ (mottam 2300 pāṭalkaḷ) acciṭṭu ulakiṉ pārvikku mutalil vantatu 1880 - 1910 kāla aḷavil ākum. avi veḷivanta akkaṇamē (atuvari eḷitāṉa piṟkāla intiya moḻikaḷil
oṉṟākak karutappaṭṭa nili māṟi ulakiṉ ciṟanta cemmoḻi kaḷil oṉṟākat tamiḻ ākiviṭṭatu. pireñcu āciyaviyalaṟiñar piyar meyl “kirēkka uṇarccip pākkaḷil tali ciṟanta navamaṇikaḷukku iṇiyāṉavi iccaṅka ilakkiyap pāṭalkaḷ; intiyāvil, ēṉ ulakil, ilakkiyap paṭippukaḷiṉ cikaraṅkaḷil oṉṟu caṅka ilakkiyam” eṉṟu ceytuḷḷa matippīṭṭi caṅkilakkiyap pāṭalkaḷi āḻntu payiṉṟuḷḷa evarum ēṟṟuk koḷvar.”

- cuvelapil (1960)

 “ulakiṉ nākarikam - paṇpāṭṭukkut tirāviṭarkaḷ, kuṟippākat tamiḻarkaḷ ceytuḷḷa ciṟanta paṅkaḷippukaḷ pallava, cōḻar kālak kōyiṟ kaṭṭaṭakkali; cōḻar kālac ceppup paṭimaṅkaḷ; parata nāṭṭiyam eṉa aḻikkappaṭum tamiḻar naṭaṉakkali; karunāṭaka ici eṉa aḻikkappaṭum tamiḻ ici mutaliya palavām; avaṟṟuḷḷum talimi cāṉṟa ciṟappu vāyntatu (caṅka kālac cemmoḻit tamiḻ) ilakkiyam. piṟamoḻi, piṟa nāṭṭu aṟiñar iti uṇarntu ivvilakkiyattip payiṉṟu ulaka mānta iṉattiṉ iṉṟiyamiyāta uyariya marapuc celvamāka makiḻntu pōṟṟa vēṇṭum. uccanilikku muḻut takutiyuṭiya avvilakkiyattiṟku itu vari annili tarappaṭavilli.”
- cuvelapil (1973)

“intiyāviṉ piṟa enta moḻi ilakkiyattilum illāta ciṟappuk kūṟukaḷik koṇṭatu eṉpatu maṭṭumaṉṟip paṇṭit tamiḻ (caṅka) ilakkiyam uyariya ilakkiya nayamum vāyntatu. ulakiṉ ciṟanta cemmoḻikaḷ variciyil tamiḻukkuriya iṭattik kaḻaka ilakkiyattiṉ 26350 varikaḷ koṇṭa pāṭalkaḷ peṟṟut tantuviṭṭaṉa. avvilakkiyattiṉ ciṟappi ippoḻututāṉ ulaku uṇarat toṭaṅkiyuḷḷatu.”

“aci, cīr, taḷi aṭippaṭiyilāṉa yāppiyal, akam puṟak kōṭpāṭu; uvamaviyal, ākiya aṉittilum taṉit taṉmi vāynta ciṟanta ilakkiyak koḷki koṇṭa kaḻaka nūlkaḷi uruvākkiya perumi tamiḻp paṇpāṭṭukku maṭṭumē uriya ciṟappākum.

“intiyāvil tōṉṟi vaḷarnta cevviyal ilakkiyam - cevviyal moḻip perum paṇpāṭukaḷ iraṇṭu – oṉṟu camaṟkirutam; maṟṟatu tamiḻ; avi oṉṟi oṉṟu cārntavi yalla - taṟcārpāṉavi.

 “intiya moḻi ilakkiyaṅkaḷilēyē tamiḻilakkiyam maṭṭumē cevvilakkiyamākavum, nikaḻkāla ilakkiyamākavum orē nērattil ilaṅkum ciṟappu vāyntatu; atu camaṟkirutac cevviyal ilakkiyam aḷavu toṉmi vāyntatu; paṇṭi kirēkka moḻic ceyyuḷkaḷi eppaṭiccevvilakkiyam eṉa ulaku karutukiṟatō atē taṉmi vāyntatu tamiḻk kaḻaka ilakkiyam. uyirt tuṭippuḷḷa ilakkiyaṅkaḷ iṉṟum uruvākum moḻiyākavum tamiḻ uḷḷatu.

“tamiḻc cevvilakkiyam (caṅka ilakkiyam) makkaḷip paṟṟiya ilakkiyam; makkaḷ uruvākkiyatu – āṉāl “nāṭṭuppuṟa” ilakkiyamaṉṟu” 	
- cuvelapil (1973) 

“vāḻkki iṉiyatu; makiḻcci taruvatu. cāvu el lōrukkum varuvatu. ivvulaka vāḻviliruntu viṭutali peṟa vēṇṭum eṉṟa muyaṟciyellām illi. vaḷḷaṇmiyum nayaṉmiyum iyalpāka amikiṉṟaṉa - ātaṉiya (āṉmīka) nalaṉukkākavō kaḻuvāyākavō alla. iṟantapiṉṉar vīrarum cāṉṟōrum tuṟakkattil niliyāka vāḻvar; tīyōr mīḷānirayam eytuvar eṉak karutiṉar. nila ulakil pukaḻi niṟīic ceṉṟavar tuṟakkattil iṉiyavāḻvu eṉṉum vekumati peṟuvar: kātal, puṇarcci, tirumaṇam, makkaṭpēṟu ivi mānta vāḻvil niṟivu peṟuvataṟkāṉa vaḻimuṟikaḷ eṉak karutappaṭṭaṉa. vaḷḷaṉmi, vīracceyal, piṟarkkuriyāḷaṉāka vāḻtal - ivaṟṟil oruvaṉ celaviṭṭa nāṭkaḷi niṉittup pulavarum piṟarum kūṟum pukaḻ moḻikaḷ maṭṭumē oruvaṉ cāvukkup piṉṉarniliniṟpaviyākum.” 
- cuvelapil (1974)

“kaḻaka ilakkiyaṅkaḷ ekkālattukkum poruntum aḻakiyal kūṟukaḷ, viḻumaṅkaḷ ākiyavaṟṟi nirampak koṇṭavi. īrāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉṉar yātta avvilakkiyaṅkaḷ piṟkālattil (atāvatu aṇmikkālat tiṟaṉāyvāḷar) vakuttuḷḷa ilakkiya nayakkōṭpāṭukaḷiṉ paṭiyum uyarniliyil ēṟkappaṭuvaṉavāka uḷḷaṉa.

“kaḻaka ilakkiyattip payilum oru kumukāyamē (taṉi napar maṭṭumalla) avvilakkiya viḻumaṅkaḷi eyta iyalum vakiyil, eyta viḻiyum vakiyil avvilakkiyam amintuḷḷatu. eṉavē tāṉ ikkāla ilakkiyat tiṟaṉāyvāḷarum pōṟṟum vakiyil ulaka māntar aṉivarum matikkat takka eṉṟu muḷa (cirañcīvi) ttaṉmi koṇṭuḷḷatu kaḻakilakkiyam”
- cuvelapil (1974)

“paḻantamiḻ ilakkiyamum paṇpāṭum paṭitta akam - puṟakkōṭpāṭukaḷ ulakilēyē taṉittaṉmiyum ciṟappum vāyntavi. cila poruṇmikaḷil tamiḻilakkiyattukkum camaṟkiruta ilakkiyattukkum oppumi kāṇappaṭalām. paṭṭāl eṉṉa? camaṟkiruta ilakkiyattiliruntu tāṉ tamiḻ ilakkiyam kaṭaṉ peṟṟirukkum eṉa ēṉ karuta vēṇṭum? (nērmāṟākavum nikaḻntirukkalām aṉṟō?)”
- cuvelapil (1975)

“potuvāṉa ceytiyit teḷivāka, muḻumiyāṉa nuṇṇiya pārviyil nōkki, eḷiya āṉāl vayiram pōṉṟa coṟkaḷil, āḻnta poruḷuṭaṉ taruvatē ciṟanta pāṭal eṉa ikkālattil karutappaṭukiṟatu. veḷippaṭiyāṉa orē ceytiyit taruvativiṭa aṭukkaṭukkāṉa pala poruḷit taravallatāṉa coṟceṭṭumikka pāṭalē ciṟanta pānalat taṉmiyuṭiyatākak karutappaṭukiṟatu. tolkāppiyam nōkku, payaṉ, eccam (colleccam,kuṟippeccam) uḷḷuṟi, iṟicci eṉṟu kūṟuvatellām itu tāṉ;
- pi. marutanāyakam (2007)

“āriyarukku muṟpaṭṭa teṉṉintiyāvi, ēṉ perumaḷavukku āriyarukku muntiya intiyāviyēvivarikkum  72  ulakaaṟiñarkaḷ pārviyil tamiḻ āvaṇam puṟanāṉūṟu a testament of pre- Aryan South India and, to a significant extent of pre - Aryan India.” 

“mutalāvatāka, tamiḻiṉ toṉmi kuṟippiṭattakkatu. ikkālattiya piṟa intiya moḻi ilakkiyaṅkaḷukku ōrāyiram āṇṭu muṉṉar uruvāṉa ilakkiyattik koṇṭatu tamiḻ. tamiḻiṉāti nūl ākiya tolkāppiyattiṉ pakutikaḷ ki.mu. 200ai oṭṭiyavi eṉpatu mikap paḻiya satamiḻik] kalveṭṭukaḷiliruntu terikiṟatu. paḻantamiḻp perunūl kaḷāṉa eṭṭuttoki pattuppāṭṭu mutaliyavi ki.pi oṉṟu - iraṇṭu nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷic cārntavi. intiyāviṉ miku toṉmi vāynta, camayam cārāta (secular) pāṭalkaḷ aviyē; kāḷitācaṉ ilacciyaṅkaḷukku irunūṟu āṇṭu muṟpaṭṭavi.” 

“iraṇṭāvatāka, tamiḻ ilakkiyamarapu maṭṭum tāṉ (camaṟkirutattiliruntu peṟappaṭāta) intiya maṇṇukkuriya ilakkiya marapu ākum. teṉṉāṭṭil camaṟkirutat tākkam valuppaṭuvataṟku muṉṉarē mukiḻttatu atu. camaṟkiruta/piṟa intiya moḻi ilakkiyaṅkaḷiliruntu māṟupaṭṭa taṉmi yuṭiyatu atu. tamiḻ taṉakkeṉṟu taṉi ilakkiyak koḷki, ilakkaṇamarapu, murukiyal (aesthetics) uṭiyatu; ivaṟṟiṉ aṭippaṭiyil aminta tamiḻc cemmoḻi ilakkiyam vēṟu emmoḻi ilakkiyattukkum illāta taṉittaṉmi vāyntatu (unique). camaṟkirutattilō vēṟu intiya moḻi kaḷilō uḷḷa uṇarvukaḷiliruntu māṟupaṭṭa uṇarvi (sensibility) - intiya maṇṇukkē uriya uṇarvip paḻantamiḻ ilakkiyattil kāṇalām; tamiḻppulavarkaḷ paṉṉeṭuṅkālam vaḷartteṭutta ceḻumiyāṉa uṇarvu marapunili atu.

“mūṉṟāvatāka, camaṟkirutam - ilattīṉ, cīṉam, pāracīkam, arapu ākiya moḻikaḷiṉ ilakkiyaṅkaḷil ciṟantavaṟṟukku iṇiyāṉa taram vāyntatu tamiḻc cemmoḻi ilakkiyam. avvilakkiyam nuṇmāṇuḻipulamikkatu; palvēṟu pāṭu poruḷ koṇṭatu (muṟkāla intiya ilakkiyaṅkaḷil potu makkaḷip paṟṟiyum niṟiyap pāṭiyatu tamiḻilakkiyam maṭṭumē); mānta iṉattukkup potumiyāṉa viḻumiyaṅkaḷikkūṟiyatu. eṉavē piṟa cevviyal moḻikaḷiṉ marapukaḷuṭaṉum ilakkiyaṅkaḷuṭaṉum camamāka niṟkum uraṉ vāyntatu atu. ulakiṉ taliciṟanta aṟanūlkaḷil oṉṟu tirukkuṟaḷ. tamiḻc cevviyal ilakkiyattil atu pōṉṟu pala tuṟikaḷilum ciṟanta nūlkaḷ uṇṭu. avvilakkiyam pāṭit tulakkam tarāta mānta iṉa paṭṭaṟivu oṉṟum illi.

“iṟutiyāka ikkāla intiyap paṇpāṭṭiyum marapiyum uṇarvataṟkup payilavēṇṭiya ilakkiyaṅkaḷil tamiḻilakkiyam mutaṉmi vāyntatu. camaṟkiruta ilakkiyaṅkaḷil teṉṉaka maraput tākkam uḷḷatu kuṟittunāṉ virivāka eḻutiyuḷḷēṉ. kaḻakakkālam toṭaṅkit tamiḻilil pāṭappaṭṭa ciṟanta intu camaya patti ilakkiya nūlkaḷ tām taṟpōtiya intu camayattukku aṭittaḷamāka irukkiṉṟaṉa. tamiḻ patti ilakkiyak karuttukaḷ pākavarāṇattilum piṟa (teluṅku/kaṉṉaṭa/ camaṟkiruta) patti nūlkaḷilum ēṟṟappaṭṭu intiyā muḻuvatum paraviṉa. vētattukkuc camamākak karutappaṭum tamiḻ tivya pirapanta patti ilakkiyap pāṭalkaḷ teṉṉintiyāvil periya tirumāl kōyilkaḷil (tiruppati pōṉṟavi) vētattōṭu cērttuōtappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ikkāla intō āriyamoḻikaḷukku camaṟkirutamē mūlamoḻi; atupōla ikkālat tamiḻukkum maliyāḷattukkum cevviyal tamiḻē mūla moḻiyākum.

“ulakattiṉ ciṟanta cemmoḻikaḷil tamiḻum oṉṟu eṉpatu ittuṟiyil valla aṟiñarukku uḷḷaṅkinellikkaṉi pōlat terinta uṇmiyākum”

- jārj el.hārṭ 11.4.2000
(i. maṟimali vēṇṭukōḷukkiṇaṅka varintatu)

 “caṅka ilakkiyam mutal kamparāmāyaṇam muṭiyavuḷḷa cevviyal tamiḻ ilakkiyam mānta iṉaccātaṉikaḷuḷ mikac ciṟantavaṟṟuḷ oṉṟu. karuttukaḷ, ilakkiya amippu, coṟkaḷañciyam ākiyavaṟṟiṟku iṉṟiya tamiḻp paṭippulakattukku caṅka ilakkiyam vaṟṟāta karuvūlamāka uḷḷatu. teṉ āciyāvil iṉṟu vaḻaṅkum vēṟu enta
moḻikkum illāta taṉicciṟappu itu. paḻiya cevviyal ilakkiyatti aṭittaḷamākak koṇṭē ikkālattamiḻ ilakkiyam uruvākiyuḷḷatu; iṉṟiya tamiḻppaṭippāḷikaḷ (vaṭamoḻi ilakkiyam mēli nāṭṭilakkiyam ākiyavaṟṟiyum uḷaṅ koṇṭirunta pōtilum teḷivākuṇarntu koṇṭō allatu āḻmaṉaniliyil uḷḷatākkattālō tamiḻc cevvilakkiyattiyum vaḻikāṭṭiyākak koṇṭu tāṉ uḷḷaṉar. iṉṟiya tamiḻilakkiyattiṉ nām kāṇum coṟkaḷañciyam, kumutāya/ meyyiyal/ camaya paṇpāṭṭu nōkku, toṉi (tone) ākiyavaṟṟil perum pakutiyiṉ vērkaḷ ōraḷavukku caṅka ilakkiyam varic celvatik kāṇalām. taṅkaḷ paṭippāṟṟali viyakkattakka aḷavukku mēmpaṭuttakkūṭiya ceḻumiyāṉa paltuṟivaḷaṅkaḷ caṅka ilakkiyak karuvūlattil uḷḷaṉa eṉpati iṉṟiya tamiḻ eḻuttāḷarkaḷ uṇarntatākat teriyavilli; uṇarntāl atil atika nāṭṭam celuttuvar, eṉpatiyum nāṉ ivvēḷiyil collāmalirukka muṭiyavilli.”
āṣar (2004)

- “Negotiaions with the past: Classical Tamil in contemporary Tamil; Edrs Kannan M and Carlos Mena Institute Francis de Pondichery”

caṅka kāla akapporuḷ nūlkaḷiṉ taṉmi

“toṉmiyāṉaviyē eṉiṉum iṉṟum uyirūṭṭam uḷḷaviyāy ilaṅkum inta (caṅka ilakkiya) akap pāṭal kaḷip pōṉṟa nayam vāyntavi intiya ilakkiyaṅkaḷ vēṟu evaṟṟilum illi. appāṭalkaḷ kūṟum vāḻkki neṟiyilum, viḷakkum nikaḻccikaḷilum ciṟanta cemmoḻi ilakkiyattiṉ piṉvarum kūṟukaḷ uḷḷaṉa: kātalōṭu kaṉi vum paṇpāṭum; veḷippaṭik kūṟṟukkaḷōṭu uḷḷuṟi, iṟicci, aṅkatam ākiyaviyum; talivaṉ, talivi peyar cuṭṭappaṭāviṭiṉum ōviyam pōṉṟa nuṇṇiya vaṇṇaṉi; aṭikaḷ cila avi cuṭṭum poruḷō peritu. tamiḻar aṟivuttiṟaṉiṉ mikat toṉmiyāṉa eṭuttukkāṭṭu ivvakappāṭalkaḷ; atu maṭṭumaṉṟu kaṭanta īrāyiram āṇṭu tamiḻ ilakkiyap paṭippil ivaṟṟi viñcuvaṉa illi.”
- ē.kē.irāmāṉucaṉ

“akappāṭal ovvoṉṟum oru kūṟṟu: talivaṉ, talivi, tōḻi, cevili mutaliya cilaruḷ oruvar, nāṭaka māntarpōl, kūṟuvatāka atu amiyum. avarkaḷ oruvaruk koruvarō, taṉakkut tāṉākavō, nilavi nōkkiyō kūṟuvati vācakarkaḷākiya nām kēṭkiṟōm; avvaḷavē. nāṭakak kāṭci oṉṟi muḻumiyāka akappāṭal oṉṟil uḷḷa kūṟṟu viḷakkik kāṭṭiviṭum.ivi nāṭṭuppāṭalkaḷ pōl virintu pāṭiyavi alla; oru nikaḻcciyik kaṇṭa allatu eṇṇippārtta oru pāvalar atu paṟṟiya tam eṇṇaṅkaḷi nērākavō urumāṟṟiyō veḷippaṭuttiya pāṭalkaḷum alla. ivi aracavippāṭalkaḷiṉ naḷiṉam uṭiyavi; “vikalum eṇtēr ceyyum taccaṉ tiṅkaḷ valitta kāl pōl” nuṇṇiya pulamittiṟaṉuṭaṉyākkappaṭṭavi.
- cuvelapil

4. tamiḻar, paṇpāṭu, kali,
camayam, meyyiyal

iṉṟu intiya nākarikam eṉṟu vaḻaṅkappaṭum nākarikak kūṟukaḷil perumpālāṉavi uṇmiyil tamiḻar, tamiḻiya nākarikak kūṟukaḷē “intiya nākarikattiṉ aṭittaḷam tirāviṭa (tamiḻ) moḻi - paṇpāṭu ākiyaviyē” eṉpar ṭilar.

All of Indian civilisation is built on an underlying base of Dravidian language and culture” - S.A. Tyler (1973) India an Anthropological perspective.

1. cintuveḷit tol tamiḻa nākarikam

tirāviṭa moḻi pēcunar paṇṭu teṟkiliruntu vaṭakkākap paraviya poḻutu (Dravidian Ascent) kuṭiyēṟiya pakutiyil uruvākkiyatu cintuveḷi nākarikam, annākarikam 1947 kku muntiya intiyā, pākistāṉ, vaṅkāḷa tēcam cērnta intiyāviṉ parappaḷavil kāl pakutikku mēl parappaḷavu koṇṭa perunilappakutiyil vaṭamēṟku intiyāvil paravi iruntatu. atāvatu cumār 5 laṭcam caturamil parappaḷavil vaḻaṅkiyatu. paṇṭiya mecapotāmiya, ekiptiya nākarikaṅkaḷiṉ parappiviṭa atikamāṉa parappil cintu veḷi nākarikam vaḻaṅkiyatu. annākarikam vaḻaṅkiya pakutiyil ki.mu.2000 vākkil mottam aintiliruntu pattu laṭcam makkaḷ vāḻntirukkalām eṉpatu posel, parpōlā pōṉṟavarkaḷ karuttu ākum (appoḻutu ulakil irunta motta makkaḷ tokiyē 9 kōṭikku kuṟivu). cintu veḷi nākarikac ciṉṉaṅkaḷ 1500 kkum mēṟpaṭṭa iṭaṅkaḷil uḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟil cumār 20 iṭaṅkaḷil maṭṭumē mokeñcotarō, harappā uṭpaṭa –akaḻvāyvu niṟuviyuḷḷatu. annākarikattiṉ ciṟappuṟṟa nili ki.mu. 3200 - 1800 kāla aḷavic cārntatu. mokañcacōtarō nakara makkaḷ toki 40,000 eṉṟum harappā nakara makkaḷ
toki 25000 eṉṟum matippiṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. māntar iṉa (AMH) paraval, moḻiyiṉ tōṟṟamum paravalum ākiyavaṟṟiṉ aṭippaṭiyil tirāviṭa moḻi pēcunar paṇṭu teṟkiliruntu vaṭakkākap paraviya poḻutu kuṭiyēṟiya pakutiyē cintu veḷi nākarikap pakuti eṉṟu Dravidian Ascent mēlē kaṇṭōm.

annākarikam tirāviṭac cārpuṭiyatu eṉpataṟku uḷḷa mēlum pala valuvāṉa ātāraṅkaḷil cila varumāṟu :-

i. mokañcotarō - harappā iṭipāṭukaḷil tirāviṭak kaṭṭumāṉak kaliyik kāṇalām. vīṭukaḷil payaṉpaṭṭa poruḷkaḷ, nakikaḷ, parutti āṭikaḷ aṉittum tirāviṭac cārpuṭiyavi.

caṅka nūlkaḷil kāṇum kaṭṭaṭakkali cintuveḷi nākarikak kaṭṭaṭakkaliyiṉ toṭarcciyē. eṭi aḷavukaḷ; ciṟṟilakka muṟi; 4.ce.mī (= aṅkulam) kōl; 2 3/4 X  4 = 11 ākum 11,22,33, eṉṟavāṟu 11ṉ maṭaṅkāka varum aḷavukaḷ, pōṉṟavi yellām cintuveḷik kālattiliruntu toṭarntu varupaviyē (es. caṇmukam 1980; ku. vēṅkaṭācalam 1983, teyva nāyakam 2002)

ii. tamiḻariṉ ciṟṟilakka muṟiyiyē cintuveḷi nākarika eṭi aḷavukaḷil kāṇkiṟōm eṉpatic ceṅkam ku. vēṅkaṭācalam niṟuviyuḷḷār.

iii. iṉṟiya vētamatak karuttukkaḷ – avaṟṟiṟku etirāṉa camaṇa, puttam mutaliya karuttukkaḷ ivaṟṟiṟku iṭiyē mutalil uṟaḻvu, piṉṉar iṇippu eṉṟavāṟu intumatam uruvāṉatu eṉṟu karutuvatu avvaḷavu cariyāṉa talla. cintuveḷi nākarikam cārnta, paṇṭiya tollintiya (tamiḻiya) nākarikattiṉ puttuyirppē, maṟumalarcciyē iṉṟiya intu matam ākum.

(The Hindu synthesis was less the dialectical reductionof orthodozy and heterodoxy than the resurgence of the ancient aboriginal Indus civilisation)

maṟimaliyaṭikaḷ (1903, 1923,1930) pi.ṭi. cīṉivāca aiyaṅkār 1929 ākiyōr ikkaruttiṉarē, 

iv. iṉṟiya icikkali āriyar tamiḻariṭamiruntu kaṟṟatē (ō.kōsvāmi “intiya ici varalāṟu”, 1957)

v. cintuveḷi muttirikaḷ cumār mūvāyirattil uḷḷa eḻuttukkaḷ paḻantamiḻē (paḻantirāviṭamē) eṉpati ittuṟi vallunar aṉivarum ēṟkiṉṟaṉar. (avarkaḷuḷ iṉṟuḷḷa varkaḷ askō parpōlā, ai.makātēvaṉ, kamil cuvelapil, posel) cintuveḷi muttiri eḻuttukkaḷit tol tamiḻ ākappaṭittuḷḷa aṟiñarkaḷ vācittuḷḷa mātirikaḷ iṟutiyil tarappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ivvaṟiñarkaḷ ovvoruvarum ovvoru vakiyāka vācittuḷḷaṉar. muttiriyik kuttiyapiṉ kiṭikkum muttirippativil uḷḷa vācakaṅkaḷi (impression) valamirunatu iṭamākavē perumpālōr paṭikkiṉṟaṉar. (aṟiñar mativāṇaṉ iṭamiruntu valamākap paṭittuḷḷār) eṉiṉum im muttiri eḻuttukkaḷ, tirāviṭa moḻiyic cārntatākattāṉ irukka vēṇṭum eṉtu paṟṟi maṭṭum aṟiñarkaḷiṭam oru mitta karuttu uḷḷatu kāṇka :-

sṭāṉlivālparṭ : intiyāvukku ōr aṟimukam “pala vakiyāṉa ātāraṅkaḷiliruntum nām karutuvatu avarkaḷ tol tirāviṭarkaḷ eṉpatum, tamiḻukkup pāṭṭaṉ muṟiyākak kūṭiya oru moḻi avarkaḷ moḻiyāka iruntirukkalām eṉpatu ākum.”

“We assume from various shreds of evidence that they were Proto - Dravidian possibly using a language that was a grandfather of modern Tamil” (Stanley Wolpert : An Introduction to India, University of California press 1991)

jē.em. rāparṭs : peruṅkuyiṉ ulaka varalāṟu “teṉṉintiyāvil iṉṟum vaḻaṅkivarum tirāviṭa moḻi kaḷōṭu iyipuṭiya oru moḻiyic cārntaviyāka (cintuveḷi muttiric coṟkaḷ) irukkalāmeṉat tōṉṟukiṟatu.”

It now seems at least likely that they are part of a language akin to the Dravidian tongues still used in Southern India (J.M. RobertsHistory of the world, pelican 1992)

kamil cuvelapil: tirāviṭa moḻiyiyal – ōr aṟimukam “ cintu veḷi eḻuttukaḷiṉ moḻi eṉṉa eṉṟu iṟutiyāka niṟuvap paṭumpoḻutu atu tirāviṭamoḻi cārntatāka amivataṟkē vāyppu mika atikam”

“The most probable candidate is and remins some form of Dravidian” (Dravidian Linguistics - An introduction, Pondicherry 1990;) Chap VI : Dravidian and Harappan)

cintu veḷi muttiri eḻuttukkaḷiliruntē piṟkālat tamiḻ eḻuttukkaḷ uruvāyiṉ eṉpatu mativāṇaṉ karuttu, ēṟattāḻa ki.mu.700 ai oṭṭiyē tamiḻukku oru lipi iruntatu; atippiṉ paṟṟiyē acōkapirami eḻuttu uruvākkap paṭṭatu; eṉak koḷvataṟkāṉa ātāraṅkaḷi rājaṉ (2004) naṭaṉa kācinātaṉ (2004) ākiyōr tantuḷḷaṉar.  aṇmiyil kaṇṭupiṭitta āticca nallūr akaḻvāyvup pāṉi oṭṭiṉ kālamum ki.mu. 700kku muntiyatu.

2006 pipravari mātattil mayilāṭutuṟi – cempiyaṉ kaṇṭiyūril vi.caṇmukanātaṉ kaṇṭeṭutta putuk kaṟkālak karuvi oṉṟil cintuveḷi eḻuttukkaḷ nāṉku uḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟi ai. makātēvaṉ “murukuaṉ” (murukaṉ) eṉṟu paṭittuḷḷār. akkaruviyiṉ kālam ki.mu. 2000 - 1500 āka irukkalām eṉṟu avar karutukiṟār. “putuk kaṟkālat tamiḻ makkaḷum akkālattil vāḻnta cintuveḷi nākarika makkaḷum orē moḻiyiyē pēciṉar eṉṟum ammoḻi tirāviṭa moḻiyē ik kaṇṭupiṭippu niṟuvukiṟatu eṉpar makātēvaṉ.”

(“The Neolithic people of Tamilnadu and the Indus vallyey people shared the same language which can only be Dravidian and not Indo- Aryan”) : 1.5.2006 intu nāḷitaḻ.

2. camayam, meyyiyal

(iṉṟiya “intu” camayakkaṭavuḷ vaḻipāṭṭu muṟikaḷ, meyyiyal ākiyavi muṟṟilum tamiḻiya (tirāviṭac) cārpuṭiyaviyē āriyariṭamiruntu peṟṟavi alla eṉpati 1900kku muṉṉar iruntē nallaṟiñar kūṟuvarukiṉṟaṉar. maṟimaliyaṭikaḷ (1903 : mullippāṭṭu ārāycciyuri, 1923 tamiḻar nākarikam, 1941 tamiḻar matam); pi.ṭi. cīṉivāca aiyaṅkār (1929: History of the Tamils kā. cuppiramaṇiya piḷḷi (1940 tamiḻar camayam) tēvanēyap pāvāṇar (1972 : tamiḻar matam) ākiyōr uṭpaṭa. āriya vētaṅkaḷi liruntu māṟupaṭṭa, avaṟṟukku muntiya ‘tamiḻ nāṉmaṟikaḷ’ iruntaṉa veṉṉum kōṭpāṭṭiyum kā. cuppiramaṇiyar (1927 : The Madras Christian College Magazine, piṉṉar 1929 (centamiḻccelvi) mutaliyōr koṇṭiruntaṉar.

 	“aṟiyāmi kāraṇamāka ‘ āriya’ meyyil, nākarikam eṉṟu aḻikkappaṭuvatu, aṭippaṭiyil tirāviṭar allatu tamiḻaruṭiyatu eṉpatē uṇmi. Most of what is ignorantly called Aryan philosophy and civilisation is literallyDravidian or Tamilian at bottom..” maṉōṉmaṇiyam pe.cuntarampiḷḷi. 19.12.1896 kaṭitam) : Siddhantha Deepika , 11- 5; Oct 1898 P .113).

rikvēta kālattiliruntē (ki.mu. 1000) “kuṭi koli malinta āriya vēḷviyiṉ tīviṉip peṟṟiyuñ ciṟuteyva vaṇakkac ciṟumiyun tēṟṟi aṉpum aruḷum perukac
ceyta tamiḻc cāṉṟōrum uḷar. avar aruḷic ceytaṉavē; muttī vēḷviyum uruttira vaḻipāṭuñ ciṟanteṭuttuk kūṟum irukku, ēkar, cāma atarvaṇa vētappatikaṅkaḷ cila palavum; uṇmi viḻā upaniṭataṅkaḷum, cāṅkiya, yōka, vicēṭika, niyāyika, vētānta cūttiraṅkaḷum; maṟcam, vāyu mutalāṉa cila purāṇaṅkaḷum puṭkaram, mirukēntiram mutalāṉacila civākamaṅkaḷum piṟavumām.”

-maṟimaliyaṭikaḷ;
māṇikkavācakar varalāṟum kālamum (1930)

“irukku vētattiṟ “paratar” mutaliya peyarkaḷāl vaḻaṅkappaṭṭa paṇṭit tamiḻ naṉmakkaḷē ñāyiṟu, tiṅkaḷ , tī eṉṉum muttī vaṭivil vittu muḻumutaṟ kaṭavuḷāṉa civa pirāṉi vaḻipaṭum nuṭpa muṟiyik kaṇṭōrāvar.(aṭikaḷ : 1930)”

“vicuvāmittirar eṉṉum tamiḻaracu muṉivarāl ceytu cērkkappaṭṭa irukku vēta mūṉṟām maṇṭilattil uḷḷa kāyattiri mantiramāṉatu ñāyiṟṟu maṇṭilattiṉ kaṇ muḷittu viḷaṅkum parkkaṉ eṉṉum peyaruṭiya civapirāṉ mēṟṟāy viḷaṅkukiṟatu” (aṭikaḷ : 1923)

“vicuvāmittirar eṉṉum tamiḻaraca muṉivarāl ceytu cērkkappaṭṭa irukku vēta mūṉṟām maṇṭilattil uḷḷa kāyattiri mantiramāṉatu ñāyiṟṟu maṇṭilattiṉ kaṇ muḷittu viḷaṅkum parkkaṉ eṉṉum peyaruṭiya civapirāṉ mēṟṟāy viḷaṅkukiṟatu” (aṭikaḷ : 1923)

“kollā aṟatti mutaṉ mutaṟkaṇṭaṟintu ataṟkēṟpat tamatu immi vāḻkkiyi naṭattiṉavarkaḷ tamiḻaril mēṉ makkaḷāy irunta oru perumpakutiyārē... maṟṟi makkaṭ piriviṉariluḷḷenta mēlōrum ivvarumaruntaṉṉa uṇmiyiṉik kaṇṭaṟintilar - vaṭakkirunta camaṇar, cākkiyar, cāṅkiyar, yōkar mutalāyiṉāruṅ kollā vaṟattiṇi viṭāppiṭiyāyk koṇṭa oḻukiṉarallarō veṉil; camaṇa cākkiyar mutalāṉa avarellām āriyar varutaṟku muṉṉamē imayamali variyiṟ paravi nākarikattiṟ ciṟantu uyir vāḻnta vaṭanāṭṭut tamiḻmēṉ makkaḷiṉ marapil vantavarē” aṭikaḷ (1940) 

[camaṇa, puttakkaruttukkaḷ āriyarukku muṟpaṭṭa vaṭanāṭṭuttirāviṭa meyyiyaliṉ piṟkāla vaḷarccikaḷē eṉpar car jāṉ mārṣalum (1934).]

“tamiḻar paṇṭic camaya vāḻvu iṉṟiya pala camayaṅkaḷiṉ uruvākā mūlak karu mutalit taṉṉiṭamākak koṇṭa vāḻvēyākum. atil ellāc camaya mūlamum kāṇalām. putta camaṇa, civa, viṇavak karuttukkaḷ uruvākumuṉ, accamayap peyarkaḷ vēṟuvēṟākat tamiḻar neṟiyiṉiṉṟu piriyumuṉ, āriyam iṭiniṉṟu tamiḻariṉ oru neṟiyip palavāṟu pirikkumuṉ, avvellā neṟikaḷukkum vaḻikāṭṭiya mika ēṟpaṭṭa kālat tamiḻ aṟivar marapu cārnta aṟiñaruḷ oruvarē tiruvaḷḷuvar eṉpatu uyttuṇarat takka uṇmiyākum. “kā. appātturiyār; 1956 (“ 1800 āṇṭu kaṭku muṟpaṭṭa tamiḻakam”) tamiḻākkattiṉ aṭikkuṟippu.

pirākkiṅṭaṉ (1981) : intiyāvil camaya vaḷarcciyil kuṟippiṭattakkatu oṉṟu uṇṭu uyirkaḷ mīṇṭum mīṇṭum piṟaviyeṭukkum eṉpatu putta matattiṉ mukkiya koḷki. puttamatam tōṉṟiya kālattil tōṉṟiya camaṇamum ācīkavamum akkoḷkiyuṭiyaviyē. piṟavittaḷiyiliruntu viṭupaṭuvatu camayattiṉ kuṟikkōḷ eṉpatu ammataṅkaḷiṉ koḷki.”

“paḻiya upaniṭataṅkaḷil maṟupiṟavik koḷki putumiyāṉatākavum cilarukku maṭṭum terinta maṟi poruḷākavum kuṟippiṭappaṭṭu iruntatu. iruntum piṉṉar akkoḷki putta camaya cayamaṅkaḷ vaḻiyāka virintu aṉivarālum piṉpaṟṟappaṭṭatu evvāṟu? enta makkaḷ camūkattiṉariṭam iruntu puttar tōṉṟiṉārō, avarkaḷiṭiyē akkoḷkiyāka iruntu irukka vēṇṭum. eṉavē tāṉ atu virivil aṉittu makkaḷiṉ kōṭpāṭāka ēṟṟuk koḷḷappaṭṭatu.”
J.L. Brockington 1981 : The Sacred thread

heṉric cimmar (1951) : “vaṭamēṟkiliruntu nuḻinta āciriyarkaḷāl makatam mutaliya kīḻttici nāṭukaḷilirunta āriyarallāta mēṉmakkaḷ muḻumiyāka iṭam peyarntilar. attakiya mēṉ makkaḷ kuṭumpaṅkaḷ valuviḻantu vīḻat toṭaṅkiya piṉ muntiya uḷḷūr āḷum kuṭumpaṅkaḷ mīṇṭum valuppeṟṟaṉa. eṭuttukkāṭṭāka cantirakup muriyaṉ ittakiya kuṭumpattic cārntavaṉ. puttarum avvāṟē.

- Heinricimmer 1951: Philosophies of India

leviṉ (1986) : “intu camayattil yāṇṭum kāṇappaṭum uruva vaḻipāṭu intiyāvil irunta āriyar allātār vaḻipāṭṭu muṟikaḷil iruntē pirāmaṇīyattukku vantatu”

G.M. Bongard Levin (1986) A complex study of Ancient India

vālparṭ (1982) : mokañcōtarō muttiriyil kāṇappaṭupavarpōṉṟa (āriyarkaḷukku muntiya intiya) yōkikaḷ, vantēṟikaḷ moḻiyic cila kālattukkuḷ kaṟṟuk koṇṭu tamatu camaya maṟi poruṭkaḷi (vētap pāṭalkaḷi iyaṟṟiya) pirāmaṇarkaḷukkuk kaṟpikkat toṭaṅki viṭṭaṉarō)

- Stanley Wolpert 1982: A new history of India.

vālparṭ (1991) : āriya nāṭōṭikaḷiṉ muraṭṭup paḻakka vaḻakkaṅkaḷ, avarkaḷuṭiya kaṭumiyāṉa perum pōrkaḷ ākiyavaṟṟukkum ahimcikkum (uyirkaḷukku ūṟu ceyyāmi) neṭuntolivu ākiyāl, yōkam, tāytteyva vaḻipāṭu pōṉṟavaṟṟippōla ahimciyum āriyarkaḷukkum muṟpaṭṭa (cintu veḷittamiḻ) makkaḷiṭa miruntē avarkaḷ peṟṟatāka nām karuta vēṇṭiyuḷḷatu.

“civaṉ vaḻipāṭuvaṭa intiyāvi viṭa teṉṉāṭṭil tāṉ makkaḷiṭam mutaṉmi peṟṟuḷḷatu. teṉṉāṭṭut tirāviṭa makkaḷ āriyarkaḷukku neṭuṅkālattukku muṉṉarē aṅku nilipeṟṟu iruntamiyi itu kāṭṭuvatākalām. tāytteyva vaḻipāṭum teṉṉāṭṭiltāṉ mikuntu kāṇappaṭukiṟatu”

- Stanley Wolpert (1991) An introduction to India.

3. naṭaṉamum iciyum

(tamiḻar naṭaṉamum tamiḻiciyum iṉṟu karunāṭaka ici, paratanāṭṭiyam eṉṟa peyaril vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟaṉa.) 

“pāṇar tamiḻariṭiyē toṉṟutoṭṭu irunta varupavar; camutāyattil tāḻnta nilayil iruntavar; eṉavē paṇ aṭippaṭiyil aminta teṉṉintiyat toṉmi ici vaṭavariṭamiruntu kaṭaṉ peṟṟatāka irukka iyalavē iyalātu. “paṇ eṉṉum colliliruntu “pāṇaṉ” eṉṉum col tōṉṟiyatu. māṟākap piṟkāla intiya iciyiṉ rākaṅkaḷ (avi paṇkaḷiṉ urumāṟṟaṅkaḷō) teṉṉintiyat tol iciyiliruntu uruvāyiṉa eṉpatē cari. takkāṇap peruṅkaṟkāla nākarikam cārnta vāymoḻi ilakkiyamāṉatu pāṇarkaḷ maṟṟum eḻuttaṟivu peṟāta nāṭṭuppuṟap pāṭakarkaḷiṭiyē uruvākik ki.pi.1 mutal nūṟṟāṇṭi oṭṭi makāraṣṭirap pirākirutattil paravi, aṅkiruntu ivvilakkiyattiṉ tākkam camasrutak kaviñarkaḷiṭam ēṟpaṭṭataṉ kāraṇattāl tāṉ camaskirutattil taramāṉa kāviyac ceyyuḷilakkiyam tōṉṟiyatu eṉpati ipputtakattiṉ iraṇṭām pakutiyil kāṇalām. ikkāla kaṭṭattil allatu itaṟkuc caṟṟu muṉṉart teṉ intiya icikkaliyum vaṭa intiyāvil paraviyirukka vēṇṭum eṉa eṉakkut tōṉṟukiṟatu itu paṟṟi virivāṉa, āḻnta āyvukaḷ ituvari ceyyap paṭavilliyākiyāl itittiṭṭavaṭṭamāṉa muṭivu eṉṟu iṉṟiya niliyil kūṟa iyalātu. ikkaruttiṉ aṭippaṭiyil viyakkattakkatum maṉa niṟivu taruvatumāṉa oru ceytiveḷivarukiṟatu. atāvatu teṉ intiyac cātiyiṉaril tāḻniliyil irunta pāṇariṭiyē iruntu tāṉ intiyāviṉiciyum pāṭalkaḷum perumaḷavu uruvāyiṉa eṉṉum ceytiyē atu ” 

- ji.el. hārṭ “paḻantamiḻc ceyyuḷkaḷ:
avi tōṉṟiya cūḻalum, avaṟṟi
aṭiyoṟṟiya camaṟkirutap paṭippukaḷum” 1975)

“iciyaṟivi āriyarallātavarkaḷiṭamiruntu āriyar kaṭaṉ peṟṟati maṟippataṟkākavē, cāmavētattiliruntu intiya ici piṟantatu eṉa avarkaḷ kati puṉintaṉar.”

“By this fiction that Saman is the source of all Indian music alone it was possible to forget conveniently the debt the Aryans owed to the non- Aryans for their musical knowledge and practice.”

- ō. kōsvāmi (1957) History of Indian music

iṇippu I

ē.ṭi. kāmpel 1816il veḷiyiṭṭa “teluṅku moḻi ilakkaṇam” eṉṉum nūlukkut tāṉē oru aṟimukavuri Introduction  varintatuṭaṉ tamatu naṇpar paṉmoḻi aṟiñar pirāṉcis viṭ ellis avarkaḷiṭam oru kuṟippiyum Note to the introduction eḻuti vāṅki acciṭṭār. akkuṟippip pārāṭṭi avar eḻutiyatu: “teluṅku moḻiyiṉ amippu, ammoḻiyiṉ pūrvikam paṟṟiya eṉatu muṉṉurik karuttukaḷukku valuvūṭṭu vataṟkāka eṉ naṇpar pirāṉcis viṭ ellis (puṉitar jārj kōṭṭik kallūri mēlāṇmik kuḻuttalivar) aṉpuṭaṉ eḻutiyutaviya piṉvarum āyvuriyi makiḻvuṭaṉ acciṭukiṟēṉ. teṉṉintiya moḻikaḷil mukkiyamāṉavi aṉittilum āḻnta ñāṉam uṭiya avar camskirutattilum pulami peṟṟavar ākiyāl ivviṣayattil tīrnta muṭivu ceppiṭa avar muḻuttakuti vāyntavar āvār.”

ellis tanta kuṟippu
Note to the Introduction - by Ellis

teluṅku moḻi enta moḻik kuṭumpattic cārntatu eṉpatu paṟṟi ituvari āyntōr evarum uṇmi niliyik kaṇṭaṟintilar. ṭākṭar kāri Carey camskiruta ilakkaṇa muṉṉuriyil kūṟuvatu:

“hintustāṉiyum, tamiḻum, kujarāt nāṭṭu moḻiyum, maliyāḷa nāṭṭu moḻiyum, camskirutattiliruntu uruvāṉavi eṉpatu teḷivākat terikiṟatu; āyiṉum mutaliraṇṭu moḻikaḷil camskirutam allāta piṟamoḻic coṟkaḷ perum eṇṇikkiyil kalantuḷḷaṉa. peṅkāli, oricā,marāttā, karnāṭaka, teliṅka moḻikaḷip poṟuttavariyil aviyaṉittumē ēṟattāḻa muṟṟilum camskirutac coṟkaḷik koṇṭaviyē”

aṇmiyil kāri veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷa teluṅku ilakkaṇattiṉ muṉṉuriyil avar kūṟuvatu varumāṟu:

“intiya moḻikaḷ aṉittumē perumaḷavukku camskirutattiliruntu uruvāṉaviyē eṉalām. mattiya intiyāvilum vaṭa intiyāvilum vaḻaṅkum moḻikaḷ aṉittiṉ amippum structure potuvāka orē mātiri uḷḷatu; avaṟṟiṭiyē kāṇum māṟupāṭukaḷ perumpālum (i) peyarccol / viṉiccol. vikuti māṟṟaṅkaḷum, (ii) camskirutac coṟkaḷiṉ eḻuttamippil antantap piratēca makkaḷiṭiyē kālappōkkil uruvāṉa vēṟupāṭu kaḷumēyām. teṉṉintiya moḻikaḷ ākiya teliṅka, karnāṭik, tamiḻ, maliyāḷa, ciṅkaḷa moḻikaḷiṉ tōṟṟamum vaṭintiya moḻikaḷip pōṉṟatēyeṉiṉum avaṟṟiliruntu cila viṣayaṅkaḷil māṟupaṭukiṉṟaṉa - kuṟippāka enta moḻi vērkaḷiliruntu tōṉṟiṉaveṉṟu aṟiya iyalāta ērāḷamāṉa coṟkaḷi avi koṇṭuḷḷaṉa.”

ṭākṭar vilkiṉs-um ikkarutti vaḻimoḻintu.

“tamiḻ, teluṅku, karnāṭik, malapār moḻi kaḷilum marāttā, kujarāt pakuti moḻikaḷilum camskirutac coṟkaḷ perumaḷavukku uḷḷaṉaca mskirutac coṟkaḷiṉ utaviyiṉṟi immoḻikaḷil oru vākkiyam kūṭa amikka muṭiyātu eṉṉum aḷavukku” eṉṟu kūṟukiṟār. Asiatick Researches āyvitaḻ 7ām maṭalattil kōlpurūk eḻutiya “camskiruta – pirākiruta moḻikaḷ paṟṟiya āyvuri” yil kāri, vilkiṉsa pōṉṟavar kaḷip pōla tiṭṭavaṭṭamāka muṭivu kūṟavilli eṉiṉum avarkaḷ muṭivi ātarippatu pōlavē tōṉṟukiṟatu. mēlum camskirutattiliruntu uruvāṉa / amikkappaṭṭa moḻikaḷi maṭṭumē cērkka vēṇṭiya “pirākiruta moḻi kaḷiṉ paṭṭiyalil” avar teṉṉāṭṭu moḻikaḷiyum cērttuḷḷār. taṟpōtiya tēvanākari lipiyiliruntu uruvāki tiripaṭintatu tāṉ tamiḻ lipi eṉpatum, “karṉāṭa”,
“teliṅkāṉa” moḻi lipikaḷum tēvanākariyiliruntu piṟantavi eṉpatum kōlpurūk karuttukaḷ. (tirunelvēliyil ōṭum āṟṟiṉ camskirutap peyar āṉa)tāmraparṇi eṉṉum peyariliruntutāṉ tamlā Tamla eṉṉum col uruvāṉatāka ūkittu accollāl tamiḻ moḻiyic cuṭṭukiṟār. uṇmiyil kaṉṉaṭa, teluṅku lipikaḷ (iraṇṭum ēṟattāḻa oṉṟutāṉ) nākari oḻuṅku muṟiyip piṉpaṟṟukiṉṟaṉaveṉiṉum eḻuttuvaṭivam, kūṭṭeḻuttu vaṭivam mutaliyaṉavaṟṟil avi nākari lipiyip pōṉṟavi alla; tamiḻō nākari lipiyiliruntu muṟṟilum vēṟupaṭṭatu -- piṟaṅkeḻuttukaḷ aṟavē illi; mēlum camskirutam eḻutappaṭum enta lipiyilum eḻutaviyalāta ciṟappu olikaḷik koṇṭatu tamiḻ.

2. inta āyvuk kuṟippiṉ nōkkam muṉpattiyiṟ cuṭṭiya karuttukaḷ piḻiyāṉavi eṉpatik kāṭṭuvatutāṉ. attuṭaṉ piṉvaruvaṉavum niṟuvappaṭum - atāvatu, tamiḻō, teluṅkō avaṟṟiṉ iṉamoḻikaḷō camskirutattiliruntu kiḷittu uruvāṉavi alla. camskirutam immoḻikaḷi merukūṭṭap payaṉpaṭuttap paṭṭirunta pōtilum avaṟṟiṉ iruppukkut (nilippukkut) tēviyaṟṟatu; avi oru taṉiyāṉa moḻik kuṭumpattic cārntavi. ammoḻikaḷil camskirutac coṟkaḷ (kuṟippākap piṟkālaṅkaḷil) kalantuḷḷa pōtilum vērccoṟkaḷ niliyil avaṟṟōṭu camaskirutattukkut toṭarpilli.

3. “teṉṉintiya moḻik kuṭumpam”eṉṟu poruttamāṉa peyariṭṭu vaḻaṅkattakkaviyāṉa ikkuṭumpattil aṭaṅkiya moḻikaḷ: centamiḻum vaḻakkut tamiḻum High and low Tamil; teluṅku (paṇṭita naṭiyum pāmarar naṭiyum Grammatical and vulgar); karṉāṭakā allatu kaṉṉaṭi(paḻiya moḻiyum iṉṟiya moḻiyum) maliyāḷmā allatu maliyāḷam; (ceṉṉi rājatāṉippaṭattil kaṉṉaṭa Canara māvaṭṭam eṉa vaḻaṅkum pakutiyil uḷḷa tuḷuvam ākiyaviyām. (maliyāḷatti pāliṉas, pari partolomiyō ākiyavarkaḷ iraṇṭākap pirippar: camskiruta (āṭampara) maliyāḷam, grandonico Malabarica, cātāraṇa maliyāḷam eṉṟu. eṉiṉum piṉṉatiliruntu muṉṉatu vēṟupaṭuvatu cakaṭṭu mēṉikku camskirutac coṟkaḷip pukuttuvatil maṭṭumtāṉ.

4. mēṟcoṉṉavaṟṟōṭu ikkuṭumpa moḻikaḷākac cērkkattakkavi cila uṇṭu: kuṭaku (Coorg) māvaṭṭattil vaḻaṅkuvatum tuḷu moḻiyiṉ kiḷimoḻiyum āṉa “kuṭaku” moḻi avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟu. ciṅkaḷam, makārāṣṭiram, oṭiya moḻi ākiyavi im moḻik kuṭumpattic cārntaṉavalla; eṉiṉum teṉṉintiya moḻic coṟkaḷ, coṟṟoṭarkaḷuḷ palavaṟṟik kaṭaṉ peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. aṇṭi nāṭukaḷiṉ moḻikaḷiṭiyē (avi vevvēṟu kuṭumpa moḻikaḷāka iruppiṉum,) ōraḷavu moḻik kalappu ēṟpaṭṭuttāṉ tīrum. āṉāl vaṭa intiyāviluḷḷa nākarikamaṭiyāta malippakuti vāḻ iṉakkuḻuviṉariṉ moḻikaḷilum teṉṉintiya intukkaḷiṉ moḻik kuṭumpac cāyal iruppatu viyappukkuriyatu. rājmakāl pakuti malivāḻ paḻaṅkuṭimakkaḷ moḻiyāṉatu teṉṉintiya moḻik kuṭumpattic cārntatāka irukka vēṇṭum. oruvēḷi ati niṟuva iyalāviṭṭālum tamiḻ/teluṅkuc coṟkaḷiyotta coṟkaḷip perum eṇṇikkiyil ammoḻi koṇṭuḷḷatu eṉpatu uṇmi.

5. ivvāyvuri kuṟippākat teluṅku paṟṟiyatu. ammoḻic coṟkaḷiṉ vērkaḷ ataṉ iṉa moḻikaḷāṉa tamiḻ, kaṉṉaṭa moḻic coṟkaḷiṉ vērkaḷip pōṉṟē uḷḷaṉa; (ciṟcila cillari oli māṟṟaṅkaḷuṭaṉ) camskirutattuṭaṉō vēṟu enta moḻiyuṭaṉumō toṭarpaṟṟavi. teluṅku, tamiḻ, kaṉṉaṭam immūṉṟu moḻic coṟkaḷiṭiyē nām kāṇum vēṟupāṭāṉatu vērkaḷiliruntu uruvākkappaṭum col vaṭivaṅkaḷil uḷḷa vēṟupāṭukaḷ tām; mūṉṟilum vērkaḷ ottuḷḷaṉa. ēṉ vērkaḷ oṉṟē eṉalām. the roots themselves are not similar merely, but the same.

6. teluṅku vērkaḷ (camskiruta vērkaḷip pōla) perumpālum viṉikaḷē; avviṉikaḷi appaṭiyē payaṉpaṭuttalām; allatu ciṟu vikuti oṉṟic cērttu peyarc col allatu peyaraṭiyākap payaṉpaṭuttalām. perum pālāṉavi appaṭi peyarccol/peyaraṭiyākavē payaṉpaṭuttappaṭukiṉṟaṉa:-

kuttaṭamu (viṉiccol) to strike with the fist
kuttu guddu (peyarccol) a blow with the fist
naṭavaṭamu(vi) to walk
naṭaka (pe) nadaka a step, progress, conduct, manner vērkaḷip palavakiyilum payaṉpaṭuttum vitaṅkaḷil immoḻikaḷ māṟupaṭukiṉṟaṉa; tamiḻilum teluṅkilum peyarccol ākap payaṉpaṭum vēr oṉṟu kaṉṉaṭattil viṉiyākap payaṉpaṭum (allatu itaṟku nērmāṟākavum amiyum);

tamiḻ akkaṟi

eṉakku akkaṟiyilli (It is not a want to me / I do not require it) 
kaṉṉaṭam akkaṟīyi  (to be desired / to be endeared to)
pala nērvukaḷil oru moḻic coṟkaḷiṉ poruḷi viḷakkum vēr ammoḻiyil illāviṭiṉum cakōtara moḻiyil irukkum:-

teluṅku akappaṭaṭamu (to take)

ati tūmamukā nāk akappaṭinti (I take it to be smoke)
tāṉikinantamū nā ka(g)ppaṭalētu (I do not take or comprehend the sense of it)
tamiḻ: akappaṭu = to take in general, seize, obtin
kuruvi eṉakkakappaṭṭatu (I have cught the bird) 
(teluṅkil aga, agu  ākiyavaṟṟukku taṉiniliyil poruḷ illi. āyiṉum tamiḻil akam = interior; mēlum tamiḻ, teluṅku iraṇṭilum paṭu = to suffer)

7. camskirutattukkum teluṅkukkum vērccol niliyil toṭarpu illi eṉpati kāṭṭu mukattāṉ piṉ varum paṭṭiyalilē-. a, ka, pa,va ivaṟṟil toṭaṅkum camskiruta tātu pāṭa māli yiliruntu eṭutta nāṟpatu ( 4 X 10 ) vērkaḷuṭaṉ paṭṭāpirāma cāstiri (puṉita jārj kallūri talimi camskiruta - teluṅku paṇṭitar)
tokutta teluṅku tātu tātumāliyiliruntu eṭutta nāṟpatu vērkaḷuṭaṉ oppiṭappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. appaṭṭiyalip pārtta aḷavilēyē teluṅku vērc coṟkaḷ 40-il oṉṟiṉ poruḷ oṉṟukūṭa camskaruta vērc collukku porunti varavilli eṉpatu teḷivākat teriyum. [āṅkila mūlattil eḻuttup peyarppukku transliteration  car villiyam jōṉs uṭiya Asiatic Researches mutal maṭala muṟiyip piṉpaṟṟiyatākak kūṟappaṭukiṟatu ]

ippaṭi am moḻikaḷiṭiyē vērccol niliyil neruṅkiya uṟavu uḷḷati niṟuva muṭiyum eṉṟālum ammoḻikaḷil palvēṟu poruṇmikaḷik kuṟikka vaḻaṅkum coṟkaḷiṭiyē neruṅkiya oppumi iruppatāka eṭutta eṭuppil tōṉṟātu. eṉavē iti (vērccol oṟṟumi) tiṭṭavaṭṭamāka niṟuva vēṇṭumāyiṉ immūṉṟu moḻikaḷiṉ coṟkaḷiyum vērkaḷiyum oppiṭṭup pārppatu iṉṟiyamiyātatu. māmiṭi veṅkayyā āntira tīpikā eṉṟa peyaril arumiyāṉa teluṅku akarāti oṉṟi eḻuti veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷār. avvakarātiyiṉ muṉṉuriyil teluṅku moḻiyiṉ amippic curukkamāka viḷakkiyuḷḷār. mēṟcoṉṉa oppīṭṭic ceytiṭa utavum vakiyil veṅkayyāviṉ anta viḷakkam āṅkilattil tarappaṭukiṟatu;

“āntira (teluṅku) moḻiyil uḷḷa coṟkaḷ mūlaṅkaḷ nāṉku vakiyiṉa. aviyāvaṉa: tatcaman, tatpavan, tēsyam, kirāmyam.

teluṅkil vaḻaṅkum tūya camskirutac coṟkaḷ.

tatpavac coṟkaḷ

camskirutattiliruntu nēraṭiyākavō allatu piṉvarum āṟu pirākirutaṅkaḷ vaḻiyākavō kaṭaṉ peṟṟu cila māṟṟaṅkaḷuṭaṉ payaṉpaṭuttupavi “tatpavam” ākum. ammāṟṟaṅkaḷāvaṉa: cila acikaḷi nuḻittal, eḻuttukkaḷi māṟṟutal, tēyttal allatu nīṭṭutal pōṉṟaviyām. (vivaraṅkaḷi vikrūta cantirikā vil kāṇka. eṭuttukkāṭṭukaḷ piṉvarum paṭṭiyalkaḷil tarappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.)

apirā nāṭṭilum mēlik kaṭaṟkariyilum vaḻaṅkum āpapramsa pirākirutattiṉ mūlamākak kaṭaṉpeṟṟa tatpavaṅkaḷ :

bambhadu

staṉam 		tānu 			cannu 		muli
srutam 		cutu 			catuvu 		ōtutal, kaṟṟal
heard 

kuṟippu: āpapramsam eṉṟa collukku vērp poruḷ ‘citinta moḻi’ corrupted language eṉpatākum. āyiṉum intap poruḷil accolli eṭuttuk koḷḷak kūṭātu eṉṟu māmiṭi veṅkayyā kūṟukiṟār. oru kuṟippiṭṭa vaṭṭāra vaḻakkiṉ iyaṟpeyarāka ataṉik koḷḷavēṇṭum eṉkiṟār. naṉṉayapaṭṭiya nūlukku uriyeḻutiya āppakaviyiṉ pāṭal oṉṟil ikkaruttu uḷḷatiyum ‘carasvati tēvi ciṟumiyāka irunta pōtu pēciya moḻiyē atu’ eṉavē ammoḻiyil uḷḷa aṉittuc coṟkaḷum cuttamāṉaviyē eṉṟu appāṭal kūṟuvatiyum cuṭṭukiṟār. eṉiṉum āpapramsam mūlamākat teluṅkil nuḻinta coṟkaḷip pirāmaṇarkaḷi viṭa cūtramakkaḷē atikamākap payaṉpaṭuttukiṉṟaṉar.
mēṟcoṉṉavāṟu teluṅkil nuḻinta tatcamac coṟkaḷiṉ vakippāṭu varumāṟu. avaṟṟic citikkap paṭṭa coṟkaḷ eṉpativiṭa urumāṟiya coṟkaḷ eṉpatē poruttam. camaskiruta tatpavam 1/2, pirākirutam 1/4, curacēṉi 1/10, mākati 1/20; picāci, cūlika āpapramsam 1/10, “camaskirutam - pirākirutam paṟṟiya tamatu āyvuri”yil kōlpurūk camaskirutam, pirākirutam, mākati (=āpapramsam) ākiya mūṉṟu vakippāṭukaḷiyēēṟkiṟār. mēlē (māmiṭi veṅkiyā coṉṉa) āṟu pirākirutaṅkaḷum taṉit taṉimoḻikaḷ. avaṟṟuḷ ovvoṉṟumē tattamakku ukanta ciṟcila māṟṟaṅkaḷuṭaṉ, 

camaskirutac coṟ kaḷañciyam
camaskirutat toṭaramippu
camaskirutilakkaṇam - cila vitivilakkukaḷukkuṭpaṭṭu.

ākiyavaṟṟip piṉpaṟṟi uruvāṉaviyēyām. laṭcumitaraṉ āṟu pirākirutaṅkaḷukkum potuvāka eḻutiya satpāṣā cantirikā (Shadbasha - Chandrica) vāṉatu mutalil āṟu
pirākirutaṅkaḷukkum potuvāṉa ilakkaṇa vitikaḷit tarukiṟatu; piṉṉar “camskirutattiliruntu nēraṭiyāka uruvākiyatākiya” pirākirutattiṉ (prākrutam mahārāṣṭira utpavam) vitikaḷit tarukiṟatu; piṉṉar “camskirutam = pirākirutam” ivaṟṟiliruntu uruvākiya curacēṉi paṟṟik kūṟukiṟatu. ippaṭiyē aṭuttaṭuttu oṉṟaṉ piṉ oṉṟāka uruvāṉaviyāka mākati, picāci, cūlikapicāci, maṟṟum āpapramsa ākiyavi paṟṟi kūṟukiṟatu. oṉṟiliruntu aṭuttaṭuttu paṭippaṭiyāka cuttam kuṟintatākak kūṟappaṭukiṟatu; ellāvaṟṟiyumviṭa āpapramsamtāṉ camskirutattiliruntu mikutiyum vēṟupaṭṭatu. kōlpurūk āpapramsatti muṟiyāṉa ilakkaṇamaṟṟa kocci moḻi a jargon destitute of regular grammar eṉṟu kuṟippiṭṭiruntār; māṟāka laṭcumītaraṉ piṉvarumāṟu kūṟiviṭṭu ataṟkum ilakkaṇa vitikaḷi vakukkiṟār:

āpapramsamoḻi apira nāṭṭilum piṟanāṭukaḷilum pēcappaṭum moḻiyākum; atu kavikaḷ payaṉpaṭuttum moḻiyākaliṉ atu evvakiyilum citivuṟṟatākak karutattakkataṉṟu

apapramcastu pāṣā syāt apirā
kirañcayakavi prayōkaner hetvān
na apacaptas sa tu kvacit

mēṟcoṉṉasatpāṣā cantirikā iṉṟuḷḷa (camskiruta) nāṭakanūlkaḷil kāṇappaṭum mēṟkaṇṭa vakip pirākirutaṅ kaḷi maṭṭumē viḷakkuvatāl, atu viḷakkuvatellām
camskirutattiliruntu peṟṟa tatcamaṅkaḷiyum, taṟpavaṅkaḷiyum maṭṭumē. eṉiṉum pirākirutaṅkaḷ ovvoṉṟumē camskirutattiliruntu peṟṟa tatcama, tatpavacoṟkaḷōṭukūṭa antanta pirākirutam vaḻaṅkiya nāṭṭukkēyuriya tēciya Desyam coṟkaḷiyum koṇṭiruntaṉaveṉpatiyum teḷivākak kūṟukiṟatu. oṉṟukkoṉṟu tolivilirunta “pirākiruta” nāṭukaḷiṉ “tēciya” moḻikaḷ oṉṟuk koṉṟu vēṟupaṭṭamiyāl tatcama, tatpavac coṟkaḷōṭu tattam tēciyac coṟkaḷiyum kalantu antanta tēcaṅkaḷil uruvākiya pēccumoḻikaḷ. oṉṟukkoṉṟu perumaḷavukku vēṟupaṭṭaṉa eṉṟum kūṟukiṟatu. picāciyip poṟutta vari inta nilimi piṉvarumāṟu mikat teḷivāka viḷakkappaṭukiṟatu.

“intiyāviṉ teṉkōṭiyiluḷḷa pāṇṭiya, kēkaya, cūlika, tēcaṅkaḷ maṟṟum cahya (cayām) nēpāḷa, kuntaḷa, cutēca, pōta, kāntāra (vaṭakkil) kaṉōja (kaṉōjaṉa)
ākiya nāṭukaḷilum iruvakip picāci moḻikaḷum vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟaṉa; iviyellām picāca tēcaṅkaḷ;  antanta tēcattiṉ picāca moḻiyil uḷḷa tēciyac coṟkaḷ kuṟippiṭṭa tēca moḻiyiṉ taṉmikkēṟpa amintuḷḷaṉa.

“āntira (allatu teluṅku) moḻiyiṉ tēciyac coṟkaḷ iruvakippaṭum: mutal vaki teliṅkāṉa tēcattilēyē uruvāṉavi; iraṇṭāvatu anniya tēciyam atāvatu piṟanāṭukaḷil uruvāki (antanta pirākirutattiṉ oru kūṟāka) teluṅkil pukuntavi.

tiriliṅka tēcattil uruvāṉa tēciyac coṟkaḷ

“teluṅku moḻiyil evvevi tēciyac coṟkaḷ eṉṟu collumuṉṉar tiriliṅka tēcattiṉ ellikaḷik kūṟum ataravaṉa viyākaraṇattiṉ piṉvarum pāṭalkaḷik kāṇka. [ellis antap pāṭalkaḷit tām acciṭavilli eṉṟu kuṟippiṭṭuviṭṭut toṭarkiṟār]tiriliṅka tēcattiṉ ellikaḷip piṉ varumāṟu avviyākaraṇa āciriyar variyaṟukkiṟār:

‘cirīcilam, tirāṭcārāmattil pīmēsvara nkṣatram, mahā kāḷēsvaram, nālāvatāka mahēntiramali ākiyavaṟṟukkiṭiyē mūṉṟu liṅkaṅkaḷ iruntaṉa. tiriliṅka tēcam eṉa vaḻaṅkappaṭṭa inta tēcattil uruvāṉa moḻiyiyē iṅku kuṟippiṭukiṟōm. atuvē acca teluku. appakāviyap pāṭal itip piṉvarumāṟu kūṟukiṟatu.

“āntira piratēca pūrva kuṭikaḷāṉa cakala jātiyiṉariṭiyēyum vaḻaṅki varum teḷivāṉaviyum (tōṟṟam kuṟitta) aiyattiṟkiṭamillātaviyumāṉa coṟkaḷ ellām cutta āntira tēciyac coṟkaḷ ākum.

avaṟṟukku eṭuttuk kāṭṭukaḷ varumāṟu:-

piṟa nāṭukaḷiliruntu teluṅkil nuḻinta coṟkaḷip paṟṟi

appā kāvīyap pāṭal kūṟuvatu:

“ō kēcavā! āntira tēcattavar piṟa tēcaṅkaḷil vacittu anta tēcac coṟkaḷiyum teluṅkuṭaṉ kalantu puḻaṅkiyatāl ivi teluṅkil ēṟiya ‘anniya tēcac coṟkaḷ’ āyiṉa.

“āntiram, atāvatu tiriliṅkattic cārnta makkaḷ, āppakavi colvatu pōla, piṟa tēcaṅkaḷil vacittu antanta tēcac coṟkaḷiyum teluṅkil cērttuk koṇṭaṉar. attakiya anniya tēsyac coṟkaḷil cilavaṟṟik kāṇpōm.

(i) cutta teluṅkiṟku uriya 30 eḻuttukkaḷil aṭaṅkāta ha (aspirate) meyyoliyik koṇṭa coṟkaḷ: bhale palē an eulogistic exclamation; avatāru /( avadharu) an exclamation of entreaty; tāvu (thavu) a place - station,  tāka (dhaca) a hughty, high spirited man;; [ ivaṟṟiṉ vērkaḷ teḷivākat teriyavilli.]

(ii) col iṟutiyil neṭil uyir uḷḷa coṟkaḷ: aṇā the sixteenth of a rupee, navalā an excellent woman,, koṭī a flag; jirā armour[ivaṟṟil cila intustāṉic coṟkaḷ; ēṉiyavaṟṟil kaṭici uyir taṟceyalāka neṭilākkappaṭṭuḷḷatu ] kōṭiyum koṭiyum oṉṟutāṉ. navalā eṉpatu camskiruta nava (=putiya) allatu tamiḻ navam (= affection) liruntu uruvākiyirukkalām.

(iii) kaṭiciyāka kalanu (= battle) toyyali (= a woman) mēnu (= the body) uḷḷamu ( = athe mind) ākiya kaṭiṉamāṉa coṟkaḷ anniya tēcac coṟkaḷākat tavaṟākak karutappaṭṭaṉa. ivipōṉṟa coṟkaḷ teṉṉintiya moḻikaḷukkup potuvāṉavi:

tamiḻ : kala - to join - kal - kalanu ( kala tamiḻ, teluṅku,
kaṉṉaṭam mūṉṟukkum potuvāṉa vēr.)
tamiḻ : ti - to beautify - tiyal - toyyali
tamiḻ: mēl-  upward / outward - mēṉi - mēnu
tamiḻ: uḷ inward / mind [uḷḷam ] - uḷḷamu
kirāmiya makkaḷ, pāmarar vaḻakkuc coṟkaḷ

ivi, potuvāṉa ilakkaṇa vitikaḷukku aṭaṅkātavi; aṟiyāmi kāraṇamākap putiya eḻuttuk kaḷip pukuttiya, allatu paḻiya eḻuttukkaḷi nīkkiya kirāmiyac coṟkaḷē. avi citinta coṟkaḷ.appakāviyap pāṭal avaṟṟip piṉvarumāṟuviḷakkukiṟatu:

“kirāmattu makkaḷ potuvāka vaḻaṅkum teṉukuc coṟkaḷ kirāmyac coṟkaḷ eṉappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ivaṟṟil cila eḻuttukkaḷ nīkkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. iccoṟkaḷi ceyyuḷil potuvākap payaṉpaṭuttuvatilli – vaci kūṟum poḻutu nīkka muṭivatilliyākiyāl vacic coṟkaḷil maṭṭum avi payaṉpaṭuttappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.

“vastāṭu 		hari 			cōmulu
testāṭā 		kollataṇṭi 	tiṭṭaka karuṇaṉ
cūstāṭā 		kuṅkili 		niṭ
istāṭā 		cēpamananna 		ivi kirāmyōktal”

(vatsūnnāṇṭā - vastāṭā;
telcuccunāṇṭā - testāṭā
cūtcutcunnāṇṭā - cūstāṭā
iccuccunnāṇṭā - istāṭā
ceppumu - ceppamu)

mēlē kaṇṭavaṟṟil māmiṭi veṅkiyā (tamakku muntiya ilakkaṇakkārar ātāraṅkaḷiyum cuṭṭi) colvatu eṉṉa? camskirutattiliruntu nēraṭiyākavum maṟimukamākavum teluṅku peṟṟuḷḷa coṟkaḷiyum, ēṉiya ayalmoḻikaḷiṭamiruntu kaṭaṉ peṟṟuḷḷa coṟkaḷiyum nīkki viṭṭāl mīti irukkum coṟkaḷ tēcattiṉ cuttamāṉa tēciyac coṟkaḷām eṉpatē atu. teluṅkuc
coṟkaḷil perumpālāṉavi ittakiya cutta teluṅkuc coṟkaḷē. ivaṟṟik koṇṭē entak karuttiyum viḷakkalām; uṭaliṉ ceyal etaṉiyum vivarikkalām; enta uṟaviyum, poruḷiyum kuṟippiṭalām -- matam cārnta cila coṟkaḷiyum, cila toḻil nuṭpac coṟkaḷiyum tavira, camskirutattiliruntu piṟanta vēṟu entac coṟkaḷum teluṅku moḻikaku iṉṟiyamiyātavi alla.

ippaṭippaṭṭa “tūya teṉṉāṭṭu moḻi” teluṅku , tamiḻ, kaṉṉaṭam pōṉṟa teṉṉāṭṭu moḻikaḷ aṉittiṟkum potuvāṉatākum - mēmpōkkāṉa cila vēṟupāṭukaḷukkum col īṟu māṟṟaṅkaḷukkum uṭpaṭṭu āppakāviya ttiliruntu eṭuttu māmiṭi veṅkayyā cērttuḷḷa teluṅku tēsyac coṟkaḷi atē poruḷ kaḷukkuriya tamiḻ, kaṉṉaṭac coṟkaḷōṭu oppiṭṭu itaṉi nilināṭṭalām. immūṉṟu moḻikaḷumē potuvāṉa vērccoṟkaḷik (cilavaṟṟil maṭṭum ciṟu ciṟu māṟṟaṅkaḷ) koṇṭavi eṉpatu mēlē niṟuvappaṭṭatu; im mummoḻikaḷiṉ potuvaḻakku tēciyac coṟkaḷum oṉṟu pōlavē iruppatik kāṇpōm.

piṉvaruvaṉa maṭṭumaṉṟi immūṉṟu moḻikaḷukkum potuvāṉa pōluḷḷa ērāḷamāṉa coṟkaḷi eṭuttuk kāṭṭukaḷākat tantirukkalām; eṉiṉum teluṅkil potuvāka ēṟṟuk koḷḷap paṭuvatum camskirutattiṉpāl allatu tēciya moḻikaḷiṉpāl pārapaṭcam kāṭṭuvatākakkuṟi kūṟavoṇṇātatum āṉa veṅkayyā nūlil uḷḷa piṉvaruvaṉa maṭṭumē tarappaṭu kiṉṟaṉa. avar camskiruta vallunar eṉiṉum teluṅkit tavirat teṉṉintiya moḻi vēṟu etaṉiyum aṟintavar alla.

alukkā vexation 							alappu (īṟṟil
displeasure - 							maṭṭumē māṟṟam)

teluṅku moḻiyil aṭaṅkiya coṟkaḷiṉ taṉmikaḷi mēlē pārttōm. accoṟkaḷip avaṟṟiṉ piṟappuaṭippaṭiyil piṉvarumāṟu nāṉku pirivukaḷākap pirikkalām:

1) tēsyam / acca teluku: ivi tūya teluṅkuc coṟkaḷ teluṅkukku aṭippaṭi iviyē. iviyum iviyotta coṟkaḷum teṉṉintiyāviluḷḷa piṟa
moḻikaḷilum uḷḷaṉa.

2) anya tēsyam (piṟa tēcaṅkaḷiliruntu kaṭaṉ peṟṟavi): iviyum perumaḷavukku, mutal vakiyip pōṉṟaviyēyām.

3) tatcamam ( tūya camaskirutam) : ivaṟṟiṉ col īṟukaḷil maṭṭum camskirutatti nīkkit teluṅku īṟukaḷic cērttuḷḷaṉar.

4) tatpavam (camskirutattiliruntu kaṭaṉ): ivi camskirutattiliruntu nērākavō, pirākirutaṅkaḷ āṟil oṉṟu mūlamākavō kaṭaṉ peṟṟavi, iviyaṉittum atikamākavō kuṟivākavō citikkappaṭṭavi.

[ “kirāmya”c coṟkaḷi(kirāmam (camsa) - ūr; atāvatu pāmarar coṟkaḷ) taṉippirivākak kūṟa iyalātu; acca teluṅkuc coṟkaḷ curukkiyō allatu (ilakkaṇa muṟikku māṟāṉa) olimāṟṟam ceytō vaḻaṅkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. kirāmiyac coṟkaḷil 1/2 paṅku acca teluṅku; 1/10 paṅku anya tēsyam; 3/20 paṅku tatcamam; 1/4 paṅku tatpavam]

tamiḻ, kaṉṉaṭa moḻic coṟkaḷiṉ amippum teluṅku pōltāṉ uḷḷaṉa; eṉavē ammoḻikaḷiṉ ilakkaṇavāciriyarkaḷum teluṅkip pōlavē coṟkaḷi nāṉkākap pakukkiṉṟaṉar.

tamiḻi viṭa tārāḷamāka teluṅkum kaṉṉaṭamum tatcamac coṟkaḷi ēṟkiṉṟaṉa; avviru moḻikaḷilum eḻutupavar tamviruppattiṟkēṟpa ettaṉi viḻukkāṭu vēṇṭumāṉālum camskirutac coṟkaḷit tatcamamākap payaṉpaṭuttalām. āṉāl tamiḻil pulami naṭiyip poṟuttavari nikaṇṭukaḷil kāṇpaṉavum tamiḻp paḻantamiḻ ilakkiyaṅkaḷil vaḻaṅkiyaviyum āṉa tatcamac coṟkaḷi maṭṭumē payaṉpaṭuttalām eṉṟa varaṉmuṟi uṇṭu. tamiḻ cātāraṇa vaḻakkil ittakiya varampētumiṉṟi camskirutac tatcamac coṟkaḷip payaṉpaṭuttukiṉṟaṉar - pirāmaṇar perum aḷavilum, cūttirar caṟṟu kuṟivākavum.

tatpavac coṟkaḷip poṟuttavari ēṉiya moḻikaḷi viṭa kaṉṉaṭam caṟṟu atikamākavum, tamiḻ caṟṟu kuṟivākavum iccoṟkaḷip payaṉpaṭuttukiṉṟaṉa. tamiḻip poṟuttavariyil camskirutac coṟkaḷ īṟṟeḻuttukkaḷil māṟṟamaṭivatōṭu niṉṟuviṭāmal vēṟupala māṟṟaṅkaḷiyum aṭikiṉṟaṉa. tamiḻ ariccuvaṭiyil kaṉippoliha ēṟiya mey illi; camskiruta varkka eḻuttukkaḷiṉ mutal maṟṟum mūṉṟāvatu meykaḷik kuṟikka orē eḻuttē tamiḻil payaṉpaṭukiṟatu; meykaḷ tāmē iraṭṭum poḻutum ṅ ka eṉpatu pōla valliṉattiṉ iṉamāṉa melliṉam maṭṭum cērntu varum poḻutum tavira (kta; ṭca;pōla) piṟa meykaḷ cērntu vara iyalātu. ākavētāṉ mikac cila camskirutac coṟkaḷ maṭṭumē māṟṟamiṉṟit tatcamam ākat tamiḻil nuḻiya muṭiyum; ēṉiyavi tamiḻ ariccuvaṭiyil uḷḷa eḻuttukkaḷi maṭṭumē payaṉpaṭutti uriya oli māṟṟaṅkaḷuṭaṉ tāṉ tamiḻil nuḻiya iyalum; eṉṟālum avvāṟu māṟiya vaṭivaṅkaḷiyum tatcamam eṉṟē cuṭṭukiṉṟaṉar.

perumpālum teṉṉintiya moḻikaḷit tavira vēṟu iṉamoḻikaḷip poṟutta variyil vērkaḷum coṟ kaḷum oṉṟu pōlavē iruppiṉum marapu vaḻakkup poruḷ ‘pala nērvukaḷil muṟṟilum vēṟupaṭṭu irukkum. enta aḷavukku eṉṟāl - iṉa moḻikaḷ iraṇṭil orē mātiri uḷḷa coṟkaḷukku oru moḻiyil uḷḷa arttamtāṉ maṟṟatilum irukkum eṉak karutiṭa iyalātu. āṉāl, teṉṉintiya moḻikaḷiṭiyē nilimi appaṭi illi; col aṭukkumuṟi, ilakkaṇakkūṟukaḷ, toṭaramippu mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ aṭippaṭiyil marapuvaḻakku idiom eṉṟu kuṟikkappaṭuvatu avaṟṟiṭiyē oppumiyuṭiyatāka maṭṭumaṉṟi oṉṟupōlavē uḷḷatu. iti niṟuviṭa (atāvatu teṉṉintiya moḻikaḷ tamakkuḷ oṟṟumi yuṭiyavi, camskirutattiliruntu māṟupaṭṭavi eṉpatik kāṭṭa ṭākṭar vilkiṉs-iṉ camskiruta ilakkaṇattil toṭaramippu paṟṟiya utāraṇa vākkiyaṅkaḷil oṉṟum ataṉ tamiḻ, teluṅku, kaṉṉaṭa moḻi peyarppukaḷum kīḻē tarappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. avaṟṟiliruntu nuṇmiyāṉa ilakkaṇak kūṟukaḷilum, ceyappāṭṭu viṉi īṟukaḷ, kālaṅkāṭṭum īṟukaḷ pōṉṟavaṟṟilum immūṉṟu moḻikaḷiṭiyēyuḷḷa iyipu viḷaṅkum.

camskiruta vākkiya amippu varumāṟu (eṇkaḷ ellā iṭaṅkaḷilum camskiruta vācaka eṇkaḷic cuṭṭukiṉṟaṉa) 

1. peyarccol: eḻuvāy vēṟṟumi, paṉmi [ataṉ viṉi =2]
2. sēratē = he sleeps; paṭarkkip paṉmi nikaḻkāla viṉiccol
3. peyarccol: iraṇṭām vēṟṟumi alippāl peyaraṭiyākap payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭatu. sva own + iram motion
4. ruti he roars eṉṉum colliṉ paṭarkkip paṉmi nikaḻkāla (aḻuttan tarum vaṭivam) medial voice
5. iṇippuc col
6. naraka uṭaṉ tattita oṭṭu āh (7ām vēṟṟumi (iṭap poruḷ) = being in a place cērntu vantatu.
7. 4il uḷḷatu pōla: kāyati vaṭi ḷipē iliruntu 
8. (=5)
9. kīta ḷaṭipē eṉṟa col + (kñāṉatī = to know eṉṟa collil iruntu uruvāṉa) gnyah (part past active kapratēya)
10. 4il uḷḷatu pōl mrutyati to die iliruntu
11. ruja = nōy (peṇpāl) + jitah 

jayati ( to conquer )- jitāh (Past Participle pasive) 

teluṅku vākkiya amippu varumāṟu

1. (cams) peyarccol; eḻuvāy vēṟṟumi paṉmi
3. svaccaka (viṉiyaṭi) < (cams)sva own + iccā desire; kāvaṭamu to become  eṉpatil uḷḷa kā cantiyil ga eṉa māṟiyatu.
2. (Sans) nitrā sleep - itaṉ viṉiyecca vaṭivam nitriñcuccu (paṭarkki nikaḻkālam) + unnāru (uṇṭatamu = to be, exist)
6. (cams) ēḻām (iṭapporuḷ) vēṟṟumip peyarc col
6. uṇṭatamu  uṇṭati (aorist part) + paṭarkkip patilip peyar vāṭu (avaṉ)
5. iṇippuc col
4. aṭuttu varum viṉic collukku viṉiyaṭi
4. teluṅku viṉi aravaṭamu to roar aruccunnāru (2aip pōṉṟu amintatu)
9. kāyakulu (1.kumāralu pōṉṟu)
7. viṉiyaṭi adverb teluṅku viṉi pāṭatamu to sing - pāṭuccunnāru (2aip pōla)

2. camskirutattil pōl

kuṟippu: nāṉkām vēṟṟumiyāṉa ‘ku’ piṟaṉukkāka’ eṉṟa poruḷil varumpoḻutu compound dative eṉappaṭum. itu āṅkilattil for  eṉṟa muṉṉoṭṭu peṟṟu for x (peyar) eṉṟavāṟu varum. camskirutattil iti tātartya caturtti eṉpar. ta, te, ka moḻikaḷil ku vuṭaṉ, ipporuḷ tarum cila piṉoṭṭukaḷ cērkkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa:- ā = be, become āy, āki (viṉiyeccaṅkaḷ); āka ‘cey’ eṉṉum ecca vaṭivam.

teluṅku: āy, ( to join, obtin oruku, ōcaram )= a side, inclination, bias). ori ( = to join obtin) - oruku) piṉ iraṇṭum āṅkila sake -ṉ poruḷiltāṉ varukiṉṟaṉa. atuvum inta vēṟṟumikku maṭṭumē. iṉṉārukkāka (for the sake of) eṉṟa coṟṟoṭariyum ikkāriyattukku naṉku payaṉpaṭuttalām.

kaṉṉaṭam: āki, ōskaram
tamiḻ: āki, ōcaram

itaṟku muntiya pāṭṭiṉ moḻipeyarppukaḷiṉ iṟutiyil camskiruta iṇippiṭap peyar relative cluse amippu, antecident  amippu ākiyavaṟṟit teṉmoḻikaḷil eppaṭik koṇṭu varuvatu eṉpatu paṟṟip pārttōm. mēṟkaṇṭa camskiruta vācakam varum nūlil atu nāṉkāvatu koṭi vēṟṟumikkēṟpa palvēṟu viṉiccoṟkaḷi evvāṟu eḻutuvatu eṉpati viḷakka vantatu. teluṅku kaṉṉaṭa moḻi peyarppukaḷil varum viṉikaḷ avvēṟṟumiyiṉavalla; (cams) ‘upapata tvtya,’ ‘upasarka prati’ ākiyaviyē varukiṉṟaṉa. ammoḻikaḷil ceyappaṭuporuḷi ‘kuriñci’ allatu kuritu mark, determine eṉṉum muṉṉoṭṭu nirṇayikkiṟatu. tamiḻilum appaṭiyē amikkalām. allatu mēluḷḷa tamiḻp peyarppil kaṇṭavāṟu ‘āka’ (evaṉukkāka) eṉṉum muṉṉoṭṭuc collip payaṉpaṭuttiyum amikkalām. [muntiya utāraṇa viḷakkattil kaṇṭatu pōla]

camskiruta mūlattil kaṇṭa ērāḷamāṉa viṉiccoṟkaḷukku (ovvoṉṟum nuṇukkamāṉa arttam koṇṭatu) cariyāṉa teṉmoḻic coṟkaḷik kaṇṭupiṭippatutāṉ kaṭiṉamāka iruntatu. teluṅku, kaṉṉaṭamoḻikaḷil pala karuttukaḷi perumaḷavukkō, kuṟivākavō camskirutac coṟkaḷip payaṉpaṭuttiṉālaṉṟi teḷivāka, tiṭṭavaṭṭa mākak kūṟuvatu potuvāka kaṭiṉamākum. tamiḻ appaṭiyalla; atuvum (pulami vaḻakkāṉa) centamiḻil entak karuttiyum takavuṟa eḷimiyākak kūṟalām.ivvutāraṇattil smarah (=maṉmataṉ) - ākupeyarāki kātalik kuṟittatu; eṉavē sexual love eṉat tamiḻākkiṉēṉ. teluṅku, kaṉṉaṭattil attakiya col illi; “tamakāmu” eṉat teluṅkil varuvatu. kāmam lust -aiyē kuṟikkum; kaṉṉaṭa cokku āci desire aiyē kuṟikkum.

atupōla, capati he curses eṉpatiliruntu paṭarkki iṟanta kālam kāṭṭum acapta camskirutattil vantatu. teṉmoḻikaḷ mūṉṟil etilum camskiruta viṉi vērip payaṉpaṭuttāmal anta arttatti appaṭiyē koṇara muṭiyātu. teluṅku tiṭṭatamu, kaṉṉaṭa pi (b) vatu iraṇṭum vilify, abuse eṉṉum poruḷi maṭṭumē koṇṭavi yeṉiṉum payaṉpaṭutta vēṇṭiyatāyiṟṟu. tamiḻilum tiṭṭutal, vital eṉṟu colliyirukkalām. eṉiṉum avaṟṟiviṭa poruttamāṉa tūṟṟutal (potuvākap peṇkaḷ vacavukaḷ) eṉṉum collip peytēṉ. cams hnuti = to dissemble eṉpataṟku teluṅkilum kaṉṉaṭattilum camamāṉa col poṅkaṭamu eṉpatākum. tamiḻil (atē vēriliruntu vanta) ‘pokkam’ hnutiyiṉ poruḷi muḻumiyākat taravilli; atu potuvāṉa vaḻakkil uḷḷa collum alla.

teṉmoḻikaḷil ciṟantaviyāṉa immūṉṟiyum oṉṟō ṭoṉṟu ottup pārkkavum. avaṟṟi camskirutattōṭu nōkkavum ēntāka āṅkila vākkiyam oṉṟi
immoḻikaḷil tantuḷḷēṉ. (ammoḻikaḷiṉ iyalpi naṉku kāṭṭum vaṭivam ceyyuḷēyākiyāl avi ceyyuḷil tarappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa) mēlē vivarittavi aṉittuṭaṉ cērttu avaṟṟiyum nōkkiṉāl ikkuṟippiṉ toṭakkattil teluṅku emmoḻik kuṭumpattic cērntatu eṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭatō atu teṟṟeṉa viḷaṅkum.

camskirutam (anuṣṭup vruttam)

kutō pūtvā kuṭa iva vinamya tvam ayō ka(gh)nah pūtvāyōkānavat kataṉām tiryavān prahara tviṣā.

[aṭuttu āṅkila mūlamum tarappaṭukiṟatu ]

iṇippu II

tamiḻmoḻipaṟṟiya cila kuṟippukaḷ

heṉṟi ār. hāyciṅṭaṉ (1853)

(hāyciṅṭaṉ yāḻppāṇattil amerikka kiṟittava viṭiyūḻiyarākap paṇiyāṟṟiyavar. tamiḻik kaṟṟu am moḻiyilum ilakkiyattilum pulami peṟṟavar. amerikka kīḻināṭṭiyal āyvuccaṅkakkūṭṭattil 19.5.1852 aṉṟu amerikkāvil āṟṟiya āṅkila uriyiṉ tamiḻākkam itu. Journal of the American Oriental Society  itaḻiṉ 3m maṭalattil 1853 m āṇṭil pak 389 - 97 l ikkaṭṭuri veḷivantuḷḷatu. 1856 kālṭuvel oppilakkaṇam veḷivaruvataṟku muṉṉarē nikaḻntatu itu) 

tamiḻmoḻiyiṉ taṉmi

1. tamiḻil iraṇṭu vaḻakkukaḷ allatu kiḷikaḷ uḷḷaṉa. tamiḻar avaṟṟic centamiḻ, koṭuntamiḻ eṉpar; avaṟṟi nām uyarnilittamiḻ (pulamit tamiḻ) cātāraṇat tamiḻ (High Tamil / Low Tamil) eṉalām.

2. centamiḻ (nayamāṉa, ciṟanta tamiḻ) mūṉṟu pirivu kaḷik koṇṭuḷḷatu. 1. iyal tamiḻ; iyaṟkiyāṉa, cariyāṉa tamiḻ. urinaṭiyil tamiḻc ceyyuḷ nūlkaḷukku
uri eḻutuvataṟku maṭṭumē itu perumaḷavukkup payaṉpaṭukiṟatu. ittakiya naṭi potuvāṉa pēccu vaḻakkiliruntu māṟupaṭṭu ceyyuḷ naṭiyi oṭṭiyatāka uḷḷatu. 2. icit tamiḻ; ceyyuḷ naṭiyil aminta iyal tamiḻē itu. 3. nāṭakattamiḻ; potumakkaḷ muṉ naṭattum nāṭakaṅkaḷil varum eḷiya naṭippāṭalkaḷi eḻutap payaṉpaṭum iyal tamiḻi itu kuṟikkiṟatu.

3.centamiḻi viṭac ceṟivu. colvaḷam, ekkaruttiyum veḷiyiṭum āṟṟal, iṉimi ākiyataṉmikaḷik koṇṭa moḻi vēṟu etuvum irukka iyalātu. tamiḻaṟiñarkaḷ tam nūlkaḷi immoḻiyilēyē eḻutukiṉṟaṉar.

4. koṭuntamiḻ eṉpatu potumakkaḷ aṉṟāṭa vaḻakkil payaṉpaṭuttum merukūṭṭappaṭāta tamiḻ ‘koṭuntamiḻ (koṭiya tamiḻ) eṉṟu antavaḻakkic cuṭṭuvatu poruttamalla. nalla muṟiyil pēcum poḻutum eḻutum poḻutum atu iṉimiyum nayamum vāynta tākavē uḷḷatu - tamiḻp pulavarkaḷuṭiya “aṟappaṭitta” cevikaḷukkut tavira. ekkaruttiyum collakkūṭiya tiṭpa nuṭpattip poṟuttavariyil centamiḻ aḷavukku atu vaḷam peṟṟatu alla; eṉiṉum ittamiḻilum nuṭpam, āṟṟal, aḻaku ākiyavi miḷirac colla iyalāta entak karuttum illi. entak kuṟiyumiṉṟi āṟṟoḻukkāka nayamākappēca, eḻutak kūṭiyatākavē atu uḷḷatu.

5. centamiḻ vaḻakkum koṭuntamiḻ vaḻakkum mika vēṟupaṭṭavi. cātāraṇap pēccu/ eḻuttu vaḻakkut tamiḻil muṟṟilum vallavaṉ (kaṭumiyāṉa pulami naṭic) centamiḻc ceyyuḷ nūlkaḷi muṟiyē kaṟṟāl oḻiya, avaṟṟit tāṉē vācitta aḷavil oru pakkam – ēṉ (cilanērvukaḷil) oruvarikūṭap purintu koḷḷa muṭiyātu.

tamiḻ vaḻaṅkum nāṭukaḷ

6. (intiyāvil) tamiḻ nāṭṭilum, ilaṅkiyilum cērttu mottam tamiḻ pēcuvōr eṇṇikki (1852 l) 80 laṭcamākum.teṉṉintiyāvil mottam ēṟattāḻa
iraṇṭu mūṉṟu kōṭi pēr pēcum teluṅku, kaṉṉaṭam, maliyāḷam, tuḷuvam maṟṟum cila moḻikaḷit tōṟṟuvittatu tamiḻē eṉṟu karutappaṭukiṟatu; ākavē tamiḻit teṉṉintiyat (toṉ) moḻiyākavē karutalām. tamiḻar itaṉit “teṉmoḻi” (Southern Speech) eṉpar.  vaṭamoḻi eṉa avarkaḷ cuṭṭum camaskirutattiliruntu vēṟupaṭuttuvataṟkāka.

ṅa. tamiḻiṉ varalāṟum piṟa moḻikaḷuṭaṉ ataṉ toṭarpum

[viviliya paḻiya ēṟpāṭṭil varum nōvāviṉ mūṉṟu makaṉkaḷākiya cēm, hām, yāpēt, Shem, Ham, Japhet ākiyōruḷ oruvaṉākiya] cēmiṉ kuṭi vaḻiyiṉaril
irukiḷiyiṉar intiyāviṟku vantu kuṭiyēṟiṉar – oru pirivu cintuveḷip pakutiyākiya vaṭamēṟku intiyāvil kuṭiyēṟiyatu; maṟṟappirivu teṉmēṟku intiyappakutikkuk kaṭalvaḻiyāka vantu kuṭiyēṟiyatu.

8. iraṇṭāvatu pirivāṉa intō – cemittiyap piriviṉmoḻi tamiḻ ākum. itaṟkup pala ātāraṅkaḷ uḷḷaṉa.

centamiḻi uruvākkiyavar akattiya muṉi eṉkiṉṟaṉar; avarē ammoḻikku ilakkaṇam eḻuti nayamākkiṉār; pāṇṭiya nāṭṭil aṭaṅkiya potiyamaliyil avar vacittu vantār. ivvāṟu tamiḻar karutukiṉṟaṉar. (vaṭakkil tipēttum potiyam eṉac cuṭṭappaṭukiṟatu. atu vēṟu, itu vēṟu). intukkaḷiṉ ātikāviyamākiya irāmāyaṇattil, kāviyattalivaṉ irāmaṉ mutalil teṉṉāṭu pōnta poḻutu avaṉ akattiyaṉi riṣikaḷ (muṉikaḷ) kuḻuviṉariṭiyē teṉṉāṭṭil kaṇṭatāka varukiṟatu; eṉavē kuṟinta aḷavu ki.mu. 1200 liruntē teṉṉintiya moḻiyākac tamiḻ vaḻaṅkiyatu uṟutiyākiṟatu. antak kālak kaṭṭattilēyē tamiḻ uyariya ceyyuḷ naṭi vāynta centamiḻāka vaḻaṅkiyatāki yāl, ataṟku muṉṉarum mikap pala āṇṭukaḷ potu makkaḷ pēccu vaḻakku moḻiyākat tamiḻ iruntirukkavēṇṭum.

9. tamiḻ nāṭṭavarākiya perumpulavar, aṟiñaruḷ tali ciṟantavarkaḷ taṅkaḷ centamiḻ vaḻakkut tantiyākiya akattiyar appoḻutu tamiḻē aṅkum nāṭṭu moḻiyāka vaḻaṅkiya vaṭa nāṭṭil iruntuvantavar eṉṟum, aṅkē camaskirutattiyum avar kaṟṟuk koṇṭār eṉṟum karutu kiṉṟaṉar. vaṭa intiyāvil iṉṟum vāḻntu varum paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷiṉ moḻikaḷukkum tamiḻukkum iṭiyil uḷḷa nerukkampaṟṟi aṇmiyil veḷivantuḷḷa uṇmikaḷ avarkaḷ karutti ātarikkiṉṟaṉa; eṉavē aṉittintiyāvilum paṇṭu vaḻaṅkiya ātimoḻi tamiḻē eṉpatu eṉpatu valuvāka niṟuvappaṭukiṟatu. (Tamil was the aboriginal language of all India). intukkaḷ [intō āriya moḻikku mutaṉmi tantavarkaḷ eṉṟa poruḷil] vaṭamēṟku intiyāviliruntu toṭaṅki innāṭṭu ātikkuṭikaḷi veṉṟu tam kīḻp paṭuttiyatu pōla, moḻittuṟiyilum vaṭanāṭṭil paṇṭu vaḻaṅkiya tamiḻ moḻiyiyum tam kīḻppaṭuttiṉar; avar kaḷuṭiya ittakiya ātikkam vaṭamēṟku intiyāvilum vaṭa intiyāvilum ēṟattāḻa muḻumipeṟṟatu; āṉāl teṉṉintiyāvil appaṭiyilli.

10. mēṟcoṉṉataṟku viviliyam 1 irājākkaḷ X 22 -l varum

“22. rājāvukkuc camuttirattilē īrāmiṉ kappal kaḷōṭē kūṭat tar ṣīciṉ kappalkaḷum iruntatu; tarṣīciṉ kappalkaḷ mūṉṟu varuṣattukku oru taram poṉṉiyum veḷḷiyiyum yāṉit tantaṅkaḷiyum (Shen habbim Habb iṉpaṟkaḷ”) kuraṅkukaḷiyum, mayilkaḷiyum koṇṭu eṉṉum vacaṉattil ātāram uḷḷatu. avvacaṉattil kuṟittuḷḷa aintu poruḷkaḷumē orē iṭattil oru cērak kiṭikkum iṭam ilaṅki - teṉṉintiyā maṭṭumē; vēṟu eṅkumalla. aṅku mayilik kuṟikkum tuki (tōki) tūyatamiḻccol, accol camaskirutattilum illi; tamiḻiya moḻiyallāta vēṟu intiya moḻi etilum illi. (camaskiruta Sikhi  tāṉ itu eṉkiṉṟaṉar cilamoḻiyiyalāḷar; āṉāl anca camaskirutac colli ciki eṉṟa vaṭivil piṟkālattil tamiḻum kaṭaṉ peṟṟu vaḻaṅkukiṟatu. tōkiyum cikiyum oru poruḷ paṟṟiya vevvēṟu coṟkaḷ eṉpatu tamiḻaṟiñar evarukkum teriyum) kapi eṉṉum epirēyac col (ape eṉṟu potuvāka viviliya āṅkilap peyarppukaḷ kuṟitta pōtilum) kuraṅkik kuṟikkat tamiḻil appaṭiyē vaḻaṅkukiṟatu; atu camaskirutattilum uḷḷatu. āyiṉum cālamoṉ kālattiṟku muṉṉarē camaskirutam teṉṉintiyāviṟku vantuviṭṭatu ākiyāl tamiḻ mūlaccollē epirēyattil ēṟiyuḷḷatu eṉalām. ‘tōki’ tamiḻ ākiyāl kapiyum tamiḻākavē irukkum. mēṟcoṉṉa vacaṉattil varum ‘kapi’ [ekiptiya moḻiyil Kaf - ape eṉpatāl] āṅkilattil ape eṉṟu moḻi peyarkkappaṭṭapōtilum ‘kuraṅku’ eṉpatē cari. tamiḻil kapi kuraṅkiyē kuṟikkum. ‘kapi’ camaskirutattilum ēṟiyuḷḷatu. cālamaṉ kālattukku muṉṉālēyē camaskirutam teṉ intiyāviṟkuḷ - vantuviṭṭapaṭiyāl atu nikaḻn tirukkalām. tūyatamiḻc collāṉa ‘kapi’ yiyē (tōki pōṉṟu) epirēyam payaṉpaṭuttukiṟatu eṉak karutalām. yāṉit tantattik kuṟippiṭa Shen habbim = habb ṉ paṟkaḷ (tooth of elephants) eṉṟa col varukiṟatu. yāṉiyik kuṟikka tamiḻilum camskirutattilum muṟiyē ipam Ibham eṉṉum coṟkaḷum payaṉpaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ipporuḷkaḷi yellām ennāṭṭiliruntu tarṣīs kappalkaḷil cālamaṉ koṇarntāṉ eṉpatiyum, emmoḻiyēpacum makkaḷiṭam iruntu avaṟṟik koḷmutal ceytu vantāṉ eṉpatiyum terivippaṉavāka ivvivaraṅkaḷ uḷḷaṉa.

11. teṉṉintiyāviṉ tolkuṭikaḷiṉ moḻi tamiḻē eṉpati nilināṭṭa vēṟu cāṉṟukaḷum uḷa. teṉṉāṭṭu ūrppeyarkaḷ, nilattiṇip peyarkaḷ ellām
(camaskirutattuṭaṉ toṭarpēyillāta) tūya tamiḻp peyarkaḷākavē uḷḷaṉa. pirāmaṇiya camaya ātikkattāl appeyarkaḷuḷ pala camaskirutac coṟkaḷākap piṟkālattil māṟṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa; ālavāy - maturi; mukavi - irāmanātapuram; kallaṇi- rāmacētu; maliyāḷam -tiruvāṅkūr. (paḻantamiḻt tol kuṭiyiṉariṉ oru pakutiyiṉarāṉa cāṇār perum eṇṇikkiyil vāḻntu varum) Tinnevelly eṉpatum uṇmiyil tirunelvēli eṉṉum tūya tamiḻppeyarē. Point Calimere eṉpatum tamiḻk kōṭikkariyē.

12. mēṟcoṉṉa ceytikaḷi “tamiḻ cemittiya moḻikkuṭumpattic cārntatu” eṉṟa nilimiyi (mēlum āyvukaḷi nikaḻttiyapiṉ) nili nāṭṭuvataṟkāṉa ātāraṅkaḷākak koḷḷalāmeṉakkarutukiṟēṉ.

13. tamiḻil aṭippaṭic coṟkaḷ perumpālum viṉikaḷē. avviṉiccol vērkaḷ mūṉṟu eḻuttu, iraṇṭu aci Triliteral and disyllabic āka amintuḷḷaṉa. cilavērkaḷil iraṇṭu eḻuttukaḷē uḷḷaṉa; iraṇṭu acikaḷukku atikamākak koṇṭa vērccoṟkaḷ mikac cilavē

14. cila tamiḻc coṟkaḷi epirēyac coṟkaḷōṭu oppiṭumpoḻutu irumoḻic coṟkaḷum orē vēriliruntu tōṉṟiṉavō eṉak karutat takka aḷavukku ottuḷḷaṉa; irumoḻic coṟkaḷiyum oppiṭṭapoḻutu kaṇṭa,piṉ varum iṇikaḷ eṭuttukkāṭṭākat tarappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.  (pattuccoṟkaḷi oppiṭṭāl avaṟṟil oṉṟu ittakiya oppumi uḷḷatāka amintatu). ittakiya oppumic coṟkaḷ mēlum pala irukka vēṇṭum.






tamiḻ 			epirēyam

pāri 			to produce, to form 	to create	
aṟa (aṟu) 		to reap,		to reap, to pluck

[cemittiya moḻikaḷiṉ coṟkaḷōṭu uṟavuṭiya tamiḻc coṟkaḷāka 1856 l kālṭuvel tamoppilakkaṇattil kuṟittavi varumāṟu: appā, ammā, āṟu, al, avā, iru, iṟaṅku, eri, ūr, eṟi, erumi, kūr, cāy, ciṉam, ciṟu, cumi, cuvar, cevvi, nāṭṭu, nīṭṭu, nōkki, paḻu, pāl (pakuti) peṟu, vā (kaṉṉaṭattilum, pāpilōṉiyattilum ba epirēyattil bo) māy, māṟu, metti.]

15. eṉṟu muḷa iṟiyi eternal god kuṟikka tamiḻar atu eṉṉum collip payaṉpaṭuttukiṉṟaṉar. pottām potuvāka iṟiyi undeveloped or unorganized deity kuṟikkum tamiḻc coṟkaḷil ‘atu’ vum oṉṟu ākum. aṟiñar lut Lowth iṉ piṉvarum karuttu ittuṭaṉ oppu nōkkattakkatākum “epirēya moḻiyil avaṉ [tamiḻil ‘avar’ eṉalām] eṉpatu pala iṭaṅkaḷil meyyāṉa karttari (tēvaṉi) kuṟikkiṟatu. upākamam Deuteronomy xxxii. 39; ēcāyā xiii. 10,13; XIviii.12; kuṟippāka caṅkītam Cii. 27”



tamiḻ ilakkaṇattiṉ taṉmikaḷ

16. tamiḻ viṉiccoṟkaḷ kāṭṭum kālaṅkaḷ mūṉṟu: iṟappu, nikaḻvu, etirvu. iṭam, eṇ, pāl ākiyavaṟṟi viṉic col vikutikaḷ kāṭṭukiṉṟaṉa. (pālvēṟupāṭṭi (i)paṭarkki uyartiṇi orumi viṉiyum, (ii)paṭarkki aḵṟiṇippaṉmi viṉiyum maṭṭumē kāṭṭukiṉṟaṉa. eṭuttukkāṭṭukaḷ (eḻuvāyākiya patilippeyar coṟṟoṭarukku muṉṉar karantu niṟpatākak koḷka):-

naṭakkiṟēṉ		 I walk
naṭakkiṟāy/rīr 	Thou walkest
naṭakkiṟāṉ		 He walks
naṭakkiṟāḷ 		She walks
naṭakkiṉṟatu 		It walks
naṭakkiṟōm 		We walk
naṭakkiṟīr/ṟīrkaḷ 	Ye walk
naṭakkiṟārkaḷ 	They walk
naṭakkiṉṟaṉa		 Things walk

mukkāla viṉiccoṟkaḷukkum viṉi vikutikaḷ ōrē mātirittāṉ uḷḷaṉa. aḵniṇi varuṅkālattil maṭṭum māṟṟam uṇṭu:-

naṭakkiṟēṉ	 I walk
naṭantēṉ 	I walked
naṭappēṉ	 I will Walk
naṭakkum	 It will walk

17. tamiḻ viḷic colliṉ pāṅku mood aintu vakiyil amiyum; viṉi niliyurittal, ēval, ceyal eñcuviṉi, viyaṅkōḷ, karuttuppuṉiviyal pāṅku (ippaṭi naṭakkumāyiṉ/ naṭantuviṭṭāl eṉpatu pōl) indicative, imperative, infinitive, Optative, Subjunctive) kaṭici mūṉṟu pāṅkukaḷum mutaṟpāṅku vaṭivattip pala vaḻikaḷil caṟṟē māṟṟi uruvākkappaṭum. viyaṅkōḷ perumpālum varuṅkāla viṉiyoṭṭōṭu āka ( = to be, to become iṉ ceyal eñcuviṉivaṭivam) cērttu uruvākkappaṭum.

naṭappēṉāka		 May I walk or let me walk
naṭappīrāka		 Mayst thou walk etc
naṭappārkaḷāka 	May they walk etc

ēval viṉikkup pala vaṭivaṅkaḷ uṇṭu. orumiyil viṉiyiṉ vērē ēval āka amiyum. paṉmiyil antanta iṭappaṉmikkuriyap patilippeyar īṟi vēr uṭaṉ cērttu vaḻaṅkappaṭukiṟatu.

naṭa		 Walk (thou)
naṭavuṅkaḷ 	Walk (Ye)

naṭavuṅkaḷ (naṭa + v + uṅkaḷ) eṉpatil varum v āṉatu ṭa (ṭ + a) viṉ iṟuti uyirukkum u eṉṉum uyirukkum iṭiyil, viṭṭici nīkkattiṟkāka maṭṭum (to prevent hiatus) varum meyyeḻuttākum. vēṟu cila iṭarkaḷil v kku patilāka y um varum.

18. tamiḻ moḻiyil iṇippiṭap (patilip) peyarkaḷ Relative Pronouns illi. appatilip peyarkaḷ ceyyum vēliyi tamiḻil peyareccam ceykiṟatu. “vanta kutiri” the horse which came eṉṉum poḻutu “vanta” vil varum ‘a’ eṉpatē, aṭuttu varum “kutiri” tāṉ “vanta” eṉṉum ceyalic ceytatueṉpatit terivittu viṭukiṟatu, peyareccatti āṅkilattil peyarkkum poḻutu oru viṉi muṟṟum, oru Relative Pronoun  um uruvākiviṭum.

naṭakkiṟa (‘naṭa’ viṉ nikaḻkālap peyareccam) maṉucaṉ = the man who walks / is walking

āṅkilac coṟṟoṭarip pōlavē tamiḻc coṟṟoṭarum teḷivākavum tiṭṭavaṭṭamākavumamintuḷḷatu.


19. viṉic collukkup palavaḻikaḷil etirmaṟi vaṭivaṅkaḷ uruvākiṉṟaṉa. viṉiyiṉ vērōṭu nēraṭiyāka patilippeyar īṟukaḷi (ēṉ, āy, āṉ āṉ, āḷ
pōṉṟavaṟṟic cērttuviṭutal :

naṭavēṉ = I will not walk
naṭavāy = Thou wilt not walk
naṭavāṉ = he will not walk etc

etirmaṟi viṉiccolli “ceyal eñcuviṉi” vaṭivattiṉ iṟutiyil illi (no, not) eṉpatic cērttum uruvākkalām:-

naṭakka (=to walk) - naṭakkavilli = do, did not Walk

im muṟiyi mūviṭaṅkaḷukkum orumi – paṉmi iraṇṭukkum appaṭiyē piṉpaṟṟalām. 

ivviraṇṭu etirmaṟi vaṭivaṅkaḷum kālam kāṭṭum vaki varumāṟu:

mutal vaṭivam (naṭavēṉ) - perum pālum varuṅkālam

iraṇṭāvatu vaṭivam (naṭakkavilli) – perum pālum iṟanta kālam

āyiṉum iraṇṭu vaṭivaṅkaḷiyum mūṉṟu kālaṅkaḷil etaṟkum payaṉpaṭuttalām.

20. etirmaṟip peyareccattiṉ iṟutiyil “irukkiṟatu” (to be) pōṉṟa iṉṉoru viṉic collic cērttum etirmaṟi viṉic colli uruvākkalām. appaṭi uruvākkum collil etirmaṟippeyareccam māṟātu; aṭuttu varum viṉic col maṭṭumē kālaṅkāṭṭum vakiyil māṟṟam aṭiyum:

naṭavātirukkiṟēṉ 		I do not walk
naṭavātiruntēṉ 		I did not walk
naṭavātiruppēṉ 		I will not walk
iviyellām cātāraṇat tamiḻil vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. pulamic centamiḻ naṭiyil vēṟu muṟikaḷ kiyāḷappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.

21. aṉittu viṉikaḷiṉ “ceyal eñcuviṉi” varuṅkālam kāṭṭum vaṭiviliruntu ceyappāṭṭuviṉi Causative form uruvākkappaṭukiṟatu. ceyappāṭṭu viṉi ceyviṉi pōṉṟē muḻumi vāynta viṉiyākaamintu, atu pōlavē viṉi vikaṟpamum Conjugation  aṭikiṟatu.

naṭappikkiṟēṉ 		I cuse to walk
naṭappikkiṟāy 		Thou cusest to walk
naṭakkiṟāṉ 			He cuses to walk, etc, etc

cila viṉic coṟkaḷ iṉṉoru muṟiyil ceyappāṭṭuviṉi vaṭivam amikkavum iṭantarukiṉṟaṉa;

naṭattukiṟēṉ 		I cuse (any business - matter) to
walk - i.e. to advance
cumakkiṟēṉ 			I bear (a burden etc)
cumattukiṟēṉ		I cuse (a burden) to be borne

iv vaṭivamum piṟaviṉikaḷip pōla aṉittu iṭaṅkaḷ, kālaṅkaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟukkāṉa viṉi vikaṟpaṅkaḷiyum aṭikiṟatu. iṟutiyākac coṉṉa ceyappāṭṭu viṉi vaṭivattiṉ mūlam ‘ceyappaṭu poruḷ kuṉṟum viṉiyi’ ce.po. kuṉṟāviṉiyākkalām.

varukiṟēṉ	 	I come
varuttukiṟēṉ	 I cuse to come

22. kuṟippu viṉi eṉṉum (Appellative verb)kaḷ ērāḷamāṉavi tamiḻil uḷḷaṉa. avi potuvāṉa peyarc coṟkaḷip pōla vēṟṟumi urupēṟṟal mutaliya peyart tiripukaḷi aṭikiṉṟaṉa. kuṟippu viṉiyiliruntu māṟupaṭṭa Symbolic verbs tamiḻil kāṇappaṭum taṉi vaki viṉic coṟkaḷ ākum. avi perum pālum pulami naṭit tamiḻilēyē varukiṉṟaṉa. cilavērc coṟkaḷ, ippoḻutu aṭikaḷāka adjectives maṭṭum payaṉpaṭum paḻiya peyarc coṟkaḷ ākiyavaṟṟiliruntu avviṉic coṟkaḷ amikkap paṭukiṉṟaṉa. eṉiṉum entap peyarc collil iruntum avaṟṟi uruvākkalām. eṭuttukkāṭṭāka.

aṭi (step, foot, root, servitude - aṭiyēṉ I you
Servant uṭi (possession) - uṭiyēṉ I the
Possessor 

ivaṟṟi aṉittu iṭam, eṇ, pālkaḷilum payaṉ paṭuttalām.

23. eḻuvāyiyum ceyappaṭuporuḷiyum uḷḷaṭakkiya orē colli potuvāṉa ēṉip peyarc coṟkaḷip pōla kiyāḷalām.

koṭiyi ciṟiyēṉi aṭittāy thou who art a cruel
manhast beaten me who am a small man 

“koṭiyi”eṉpatu kūṭṭuccol eḻuvāy; ‘ciṟiyēṉi’
eṉpatu kūṭṭuc col ceyappaṭuporuḷ - ai eṉṉum
vēṟṟumi urupi ēṟṟuḷḷatu. ‘aṭittāy’
potuvāṉa iṟantakāla viṉimuṟṟu.

24. tamiḻ viṉiccoṟkaḷil uḷḷa iṉṉoru ciṟappuk kūṟiyum kuṟippiṭa vēṇṭum. atu tāṉviṉiyeccam (gerund, verbal participle, first indefinite mood) pala ceyalkaḷip paṟṟiya oruvākkiyattil iṟuti viṉiyik kuṟikkum collit tavira ēṉiya viṉikaḷ viṉiyeccamākavēvarum: 

nāḷikku nām ceṉṟu, neṟpayiri aṟuttuk katirk
kaṭṭukaḷik kaḷattukku uyttu, avaṟṟi aṭittu
nellākki, viṟṟuṇa, varikaḷik kaṭṭuvōmāka

eṉṉum poḻutu muntiya viṉikaḷ ellām viṉiyeccamāka irukka kaṭici viṉi (kaṭṭuvōmāka) maṭṭumē viṉimuṟṟāka iruppatik kāṇalām. viṉimuṟṟi yotta atēkālam  , eṇ, iṭam mutaliyavi viṉiyeccaṅkaḷukkum amikiṉṟaṉa.

cuṭṭu (ciṟappu/ potumi) coṟkaḷ (Articles)

25. tamiḻil ivi illi. (The pōṉṟa ciṟappuc cuṭṭuc collukkup patilāka cuṭṭup patilip peyarkaḷ (ivaṉ, avaṉ, itu, atu) allatu vēṟu vaki vaḻakkukaḷ payaṉpaṭukiṉṟaṉa. (a, an pōṉṟa ) potumic cuṭṭuc collukkup pakaramāka oru/ōr payaṉpaṭukiṟatu. 

vēṟṟumi urupēṟkum poḻutu peyarc coṟkaḷ aṭiyum tiripukaḷ Declensions

26. tamiḻil peyarc coṟkaḷukku eṭṭu vēṟṟumikaḷ uḷḷaṉa. (oru cilavaṟṟit tavira) aṉittup peyarc coṟkaḷukkum vēṟṟumi urupukaḷ potuvāṉaviyē. avvēṟṟumikaḷāl peyarccoṟkaḷ aṭiyum poruḷ māṟṟatti muṟiyē piṉvarumāṟu taralām: avaṉ He; avaṉi him; avaṉi / avaṉōṭu by or with him;avaṉukku To him; avaṉiliruntu from him, avaṉatu of him or his; avaṉil/ avaṉiṭattil at or in him; viḷivēṟṟumi Vocative

patilippeyarkaḷ Pronouns

27. mūviṭa naparkaḷic cuṭṭum patilippeyarkaḷum, cila peyarccoṟkaḷum iraṇṭu vakip paṉmi vaṭivaṅkaḷik koṇṭuḷḷaṉa; avviru vakikaḷumē cuṭṭappaṭuvāṉ uyarvik kāṭṭap payaṉpaṭukiṉṟaṉa. appaṉmippeyarkaḷukkuriya viṉic coṟkaḷum avaṟṟukku iyipākavē amikiṉṟaṉa:-

nāṉ I 	nām, nāṅkaḷ = We, allatu (mariyāti koṭuttuc
cuṭṭum) nāṉ

nī thou 	nīr, nīṅkaḷ = ye  allatu (mariyāti koṭuttuc
cuṭṭum) nī

avaṉ he 	avar, avarkaḷ = they allatu mariyāti koṭuttuc

avaḷ she	 cuṭṭum avaṉ / avaḷ

28. taṉmippaṉmiyil nām/ nāṅkaḷ ākiya coṟkaḷip payaṉpaṭuttum poḻutu avaṟṟil aṭaṅku pavarkaḷ vēṟupaṭuvar 

nām 		= 	pēcupavaṉ, kēṭpavarkaḷ (nām ellām 
pāvikaḷ) We are all sinners
nāṅkaḷ 	= 	pēcupavaṉ maṭṭum (“nīṅkaḷ” mātiri)

29. kaṭavuḷiṭam ‘nī’ eṉṟu kūṟāmal ‘tēvarīr’ (= kaṭavuḷarkaḷākiya nīṅkaḷ) eṉṟu kūṟuvatum peruvaḻakkil uḷḷatu. (tēvar = kaṭavuḷ + īr (muṉṉili patilippeyar paṉmi mariyāti vaṭivu) = tēvarīr. muṉṉilip patilip peyarp paṉmiyil ‘īr’ varuvatu pōla viṉiyilum īrvarum (nīr vantīr)

kaṭavuḷik kuṟikkum “cuvāmi” paṉmip poruḷil (Pluralis excellentias) cuvāmiyār eṉṟu varum. paṭarkkip paṉmiyil viṉiccol īṟākavarum ār, ārkaḷ (vantār, vantārkaḷ) eṉpavaṟṟil mutalvaṭivamākiya ār ippaṭi peyarccol īṟākaccērkkappaṭukiṟatu – mariyātikkāka 

vākkiya amippil coṟkaḷum coṟṟoṭar kaḷum varum varici

30. colloṭṭukaḷ yāvumē peyarc collukkup piṉṉar maṭṭumē varuvatāl tamiḻil avi piṉṉoṭṭukaḷē (Postpositions) ākum.

31. āṟām vēṟṟumiyākiya uṭimip poruḷ vēṟṟumiyil ‘avaṉatu māṭu’ eṉpatu pōl ‘atu’ eṉṉum vēṟṟumi urupu etu uṭimi eṉac collappaṭukiṟatō (māṭu) ataṟku muṉṉar varum.

32. ceyappaṭuporuḷ kuṉṟāviṉiyil eḻuvāy, ceyappaṭuporuḷ ivaṟṟukkup piṉṉarē viṉic col varum: nāṉ avaṉi aṭittēṉ (I him beat)
33. palavakic coṟkaḷiṉ (Parts of speech) varici muṟi āṅkilattil uḷḷataṟku ēṟattāḻat talikīḻākavē irukkum.

“The man who came here yesterday”
= nēṟṟu iṅkē vantavaṉ
(yesterday here who came the man)

peyaraṭikaḷ (Adjectives)

34. peyaraṭikaḷ peyarc coṟkaḷukku muṉṉar varukiṉṟaṉa. pāl, eṇ, vēṟṟumi ivaṟṟukkēṟpa peyaraṭikaḷ enta māṟṟamum peṟuvatilli.

35. oppumiyik kāṭṭavō, taṉicciṟappik kāṭṭavō (Comparative, Superlative degree kaḷukku āṅkilattil uḷḷatu pōl peyaraṭikaḷ tamiḻilil urumāṟṟam peṟuvatilli. oppumiyik kāṭṭum poḻutu nāṉkām/ aintām vēṟṟumi Dative / Ablative urupukaḷip payaṉpaṭutti tamiḻil “ataṟku itu nallatu” eṉpatu pōl varum. (āṅkilattil ammoḻiyiṉ amippukkēṟka) this is better than that eṉṟu varum poḻutu- “than that” il oppumic col mutalil varum; āṉāl tamiḻil ataṟku eṉṟu oppumic col īṟṟil varum)

36. “pārkka” eṉṉum viṉic collip payaṉ paṭuttiyum tamiḻil oppumi viḷakkappaṭum “atippārkka itu nallatu” (to look at that/while we look at that, this is good)

37. taṉicciṟappāṉatu “ellāvaṟṟiṉum/ ellōriṉum” eṉṉum collip payaṉpaṭutti aintām Ablative) vēṟṟumi urupuṭaṉ viḷakkappaṭum. “ellōriṉum kaṭavuḷ uyarntavar”; “aṉittilum itu ciṟantatu.”

38. tamiḻiṉ piṟa taṉmikaḷiyum virivāka viḷakkalām. āyiṉum inta āyviṉ nōkkattiṟku itu pōkum. annōkkamāṉatu tamiḻ ciṟanta, mukkiyamāṉa moḻikaḷuḷ oṉṟu eṉpati aṟiñarkaḷukkut terivippatum, ammoḻiyi ulaka moḻikaḷiṉ vakippāṭṭil evviṭattil varum eṉpatu kuṟittu avarkaḷ āyvu ceyya utavuvatumē ākum.

[aṭuttu āṅkila mūlamum tarappaṭukiṟatu ]
